1b2(1): thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das gnyan yod na/rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi 'tshal mgon med zas sbyin gyi kun dga' ra ba na/dge slong khri nyis stong gi/dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po la 'di lta ste/tshe dang ldan pa 
1b3(1): kun shes ko'u di n+ya dang /tshe dang ldan pa rta thul dang /tshe dang ldan pa rlangs pa dang /tshe dang ldan pa/mying chen dang /tshe dang ldan pa bzang po dang /tshe dang ldan pa grags sbyin dang /tshe dang ldan pa'i dri ma myed 
1b4(1): pa dang /tshe dang ldan pa lag bzangs dang /tshe dang ldan pa gang po dang /tshe dang ldanapa ba lang bdag dang /tshe dang ldan pa lteng rgyas 'od srung dang /tshe dang ldan pa chu klung 'od srungs dang /
1b5(1): tshe dang ldan pa g.ya 'od srung dang /tshe dang/ldan pa sha ri'i bu dang /tshe dang ldan pa ma'u 'gal gyi bu chen po dang /tshe dang ldan ba 'od srung chen po dang /tshe dang dang ldan pa ka ta'i 
1b6(1): bu chen po dang /tshe dang lhan pa ka phin chen po dang /tshe dang lngan pa gsus po dang /tshe dang 
1b7(1): ldan pa skul byed dang /tshe dang ldan pa byams ma'i bu gang po dang /tshe dang ldang ba ma 'gags pa dang /tshe dang

1b9(1): pha pha rold tu 'gro ba chen po dang /tshe dang lda na ba ba ku la dang /tshe dang dga' bo dang /tshe dang ldan pa sgra gcan 'dzin dang /tshe dang ldan pa lags 'ongs dang /tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bo dang /de dag 
1b10(1): la sogs pa dge slong khri nyis stong dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs so//byang chub sems dpa' sum khri nyis stong /thams cad kyang skye ba gcig gis thogs pa/byang chub sems dpa'i 
2a1(1): pha rol tu phyin pa thams cad las nges par skyes pa/byang chub sems dpa'i mngon par shes pa thams cad kyis rnam par rtse ba/byang chub sems dpa'i gzungs thams cad thob pa/byang chub sems dpa' 
2a2(1): spobs pa thams cad thob pa/byang cub sems dpa'i smon lam thams cad shin tu rdzogs pa/byang chub sems dpas so so yang dag par rig pa thams cad rtogs par khong du chud pa/byang chub sems dpa ting nge 'dzin thams 
2a3(1): cad la dbang thob pa/byang chub sems pa'i dbang thams cad thob pa/byang gi chub sems dpa' bzod pa thams cad la zhugs pa/byang chub sems la dpa'i thams cad rab tu rdzogs pa sha stag ste/'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems 
2a4(1): dpa' chen po byams pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' chen po gzungs kyi dbang phyug rgyal po dang /byang chub sems dba' sems dpa' chen po seng ge'i tog dang /byang chub sems ni dpa' sems dpa' chen po don grub blo 
2a5(1): gros dang /byang chub sems rnam par sbyor dang /byang chub sems kyi dbang phyug sems dpa' chen po dang /byang chub sems/byang chub sems dpa' skye bo dang /byang chub sems nyid dpa' sems dpa' chen 
2a6(1): po brtson 'grus rtag par sbyor dang /byang chu sems dpa' sems dpa'/chen po snying rje cher sems dang /de dag la sogs pa byang chub sems/dpa' sum khri nyis stong dang yang thabs gcig go/de'i tshe bcom ldan 
2a7(1): 'das mnyan yod kyi grong khyer chen po na 'khor bzhi dang /rgyal po dang /rgyal po'i sras dang /rgyal po'i blon po chen po dang /chab 'og gi rgyal phran dang /rgyal po'i zham ring ba dang /rgyal rigs dang /bram ze 
2a8(1): dang /tshong dpon dang /khyim bdag dang /blon po dang /'khor gyi mi dang /grong khyer pa dang /yul gyi mi dang /mu stegs can dang /dge sbyong dang /bram ze dang /spyod pa pa dang /kun tu rgyu sna 
2a9(1): sna tshogs kyis rim gror byas/bla mar byas/bsti stang du byas/mchod pa byas so//bcom ldan 'das ni bza' ba dang /bca' ba dang /myang ba bsod cing mang ba dang /na bza' dang /bsod snyoms dang /gzims 
2a10(1): cha dang /snyun gyi gsos sman dang /yo byad rung ba brnyes shing /bcom ldan 'das ni brnyes pa dam pa dang /grags pa dam pa brnyes kyang /pad+ma la chus mi gos pa ltar/thams cad du ma chags ma gos par bzhugs so//
2b1(1): bcom ldan 'das kyi grags pa dang /sgra dang /snyan pa ni 'di skad du/bcom ldan 'das de ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rig pa dang zhabs su ldan pa/bde bar gshegs pa/
2b2(1): 'jig rten mkhyen pa/skyes bu 'dul ba'i kha lo sgyur ba/bla na med pa/lha dang mi rnams kyi ston pa/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das shes bya bar 'jig rten du mngon par grags par gyur to//de ni spyan lnga dang ldan pa ste/'jig rten 
2b3(1): 'di dang 'jig rten pha rol lha dang bcas pa/bdud dang bcas pa/tshangs pa dang bcas pa/dge sbyong dang bram zer bcas pa'i skye dgu lha dang mir bcas pa dag rang gis mngon par mkhyen pas mngon du mdzad nas/nye bar bsgrubs te 
2b4(1): rab tu ston to//de thog mar dge ba/bar du dge ba/tha mar dge ba/don bzang po/tshig 'bru bzang po/ma 'dres pa/yongs su rdzogs pa/yongs su dag pa/yongs su dkar ba'i chos ston te/tshangs pa'i 
2b5(1): spyod pa yang dag par ston to//de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis nam gyi gud thun la/sangs rgyas kyi rgyan bkod pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs par gyur to//bcom ldan 'das kyis sangs rgyas kyi 
2b6(1): rgyan bkod pa zhes bya ba'iting nge 'dzin de la snyoms par zhugs ma thag tu/skad cig de nyid la bcom ldan 'das kyi dbu'i gtsug gi gtsug tor gyi gseb nas/sngon gyi sangs rgyas rjes su dran par bya ba la 
2b7(1): chags pa med pa'i ye shes snang ba zhes bya ba'i 'od zer byung ste/des gnas gtsang ma'i lha'i gnas thams cad snang bar byas nas/lha'i bu dbang phyug chen po la sogs pa lha'i bu tshad med pa dag yang dag par bskul to//de bzhin 
2b8(1): gshegs pa'i 'od zer gyi tshogs de las bskul ba'i tshogs su bcad pa 'di dag kyang byung ngo //ye shes 'od ldan mun pa com 'od mdzad pa//'od bzang gzi brjid dam pa dri med pa//sku lus rab zhi thugs dge zhi ba po//
2b9(1): shAkya'i seng ge thub dang phrad par gyis//ye shes rgya mtsho mthu chen dag pa ste//chos kyi mnga' bdag kun mkhyen thub pa'i dbang //mi dang lhas mchod lha yi lhar gyur pa//chos la rab byung mnga' dbang mdzad 
2b10(1): la rton//gang zhig gdul dka'i sems la mnga' mdzad de//gang zhig bdud kyi snyi las grol ba'i thugs//de 'dir mthong thos don med ma yin gyis//rnam grol mthar phyin zhi ba'i drung du deng //
3a1(1): mtshungs med chos la snang bar gyur pa ste//mun sel tshul bzang ston par rab mdzad pa//sangs rgyas spyod zhi mkhyen pa dpag med pa//de yi drung du dad 
3a2(1): pa mchog gis dong //de ni sman pa'i rgyal po bdud rtsi gtong //de ni smra ba dpa' bo tshogs ngan 'joms//de ni chos kyi rtsa lag don dam mkhas//de ni 'dren pa bla med lam ston pa'o//
3a3(1): gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu de dag sngon gyi sangs rgyas rjes su dran par bya ba la chags pa med pa'i ye shes snang ba'i 'od zer des phog cing /tshigs su bcad pa rnam pa de lta bu de dag gis 
3a4(1): kyang bskul ma thag tu rab tu zhi ba'i ting nge 'dzin de las las sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gi sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi yon tan bkod pa gang yin pa dag dang /'khor gyi dkyil 'khor

3a6(1): lha'i bu dbang phyug ces bya ba dang /dbang phyug chen po dang /dga' bo dang /shin tu dga' bo dang /tsan+dan dang /mchod byas dang /rab tu zhi ba dang /dul 
3a7(1): ba'i dbang phyug la sogs pa de dag dang /gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu gzhan shin tu mang po kha dog 'phags shing /shin tu 'phags pa dang ldan pa dag gis/lha'i snang bas rgyal bu rgyal byed 
3a8(1): kyi tshal thams cad snang bar byas te/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der dong nas phyin pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te/phyogs gcig tu 'khod do//phyogs

6a2(1): kyi yan lag dang /bsam gtan gyi ze ba can ting nge 'dzind kyi ze ba'i rdul dang bral ba/yon tan gyi tshogs rgya chen po'i 'tsho ba las skyes pa'i nga rgyal dang dregs pa'i bdung ba myed pa'i zla bas dri myed pa'i 'dab ma 
6a3(1): brgyad pa/tshul khrims dang thos pa dang bag yod pa'i gsung gi dang phyogs bcur thogs pa myed par snang ba/'jig rten shes pas thub par gyurd/'jig rten gyi chos brgyad kyis ma gos pa/skyes bu chen 
6a4(1): po'i dam pa/bsod nams dang /ye shes kyi tshogs kyi dri zhim po 'byung ba/shes rab dang /ye shes kyi nyi ma'i 'od zer gyis myig shind tu rnam par dag pa'i pad ma brgyad pa rgyas pa/rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa ba'i 
6a5(1): mchog tu mgyogs pa'i shugs dang ldan pa/'phags pa'i bden pa bzhi'i sder mo dang che ba shind tu rno ba tshangs pa'i gnas la bzhi'i so drang ba snyedad pa/bsdu ba'i dngos po bzhis shind tu 
6a6(1): sdud pa'i mgo dang ldan pa/rtend cing 'breld par 'byung ba'i yan lag bcu gnyis pa khong du chud pas rim gyis 'phags pa'i lus dang ldan pa byang chub kyi phyogs kyi chos sum cu rtsa 
6a7(1): bdun yang dag par yongs su rdzogs pas ral pa can du byas shing rig pa dang ye shes kyi ze ba can/rnam par thard pa'i sgo gsum gyi kha gdangs pa/zhi gnas dang lhag mthong shind tu rnam 
6a8(1): par dag pa'i myig dang ldan pa/bsam gtan rnam par thar pa dang /ting nge 'dzind dang snyoms par 'jug pa'i ri sul gyi phug na gnas pa/spyod lam bzhi dang /'dul ba'i nags tshal dang /
6a9(1): nags 'dab nas skyes pa'i lus dang ldan pa/stobs bcu dang /myi 'jigs pa bzhi dang nye ba'i stobs dang ldan pa/skye ba dang 'jig pa'i 'jigs pa dang spu g.yo'i ba myed pa dang rtsal la zhum pa myed 
6a10(1): pa dang /mu stegs can gyi bong bu dang ri dgas mang po'i tshogs rab tu 'jom pa/bdag myed par sgra brjod pa'i seng ge'i nga ro chen po sgrog pa/skyes bu seng ge rnam par rold pa dang /bsam gtan gyi dkyil 
6b1(1): 'khor las shes rab kyi 'od dang/'od zer gyis mu stegs bye ba srin bu mye khyer gyi tshogs 'od myed par byed pa/ma rig pa'i mun pa mun nag gi ling tog dang rab rib myed par byed pa/stobs dang brtson 'grus 'bra
6b2(1): ba lha dang myi'i nang du bsod nams kyi gzi brjid kyi gzi dang ldan ba/skyes bu chen po'i nyi ma/nag po'i phyogs myed pa/kar po'i phyogs yongs su rdzogs pa/blta na sdug la yid du yod du 'ong ba/dmyig
6b3(1): gi dbang po la thogs pa myed pa/lha brgya stong gisskar ma'i tshogs kyis shind tu rgyand pa/bsam gtan dang/rnam par thard pa dang/ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor can/byang chub kyi yan lag gis bde' ba
6b4(1): bas zla ba'i 'od zer du gyurd pas/myi dang lha mkhas pa'i me tog ku mu da kha 'byed pa/skyes bu chen po zla ba/'khord kyi gling bzhir song ba/byang chub kyi yan lag bdun gyi rin po che dang ldan ba/
6b5(1): sems can thams cad la sems mnyam bar sbyor ba/blo la thogs pa myed pa/dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam gyi dka' thub dang brtul zhugs shind tu phun sum tshogs shing rab tu rdzogs pas khyad bar du
6b6(1): sogs 'byung bar byis ba'i bsam ba can/chos kyi rgyal po'i chos kyi 'khor lo rin po ri mchog dam pa thogs pa myed par bskor ba/'khor los sgyur ba'i rigs kyi rgyud tu 'byung ba dang/chos kyi 
6b7(1): 'khor los sgyur ba chen po rtend cing 'breld par 'byung ba zab cing gting dpag dka' ba'i chos rin po che thams cad kyis rab tu gang ba thos pas myi ngoms pas yangs shing rgya che la mtha' yas pa'i ye shes dang/
6b8(1): tshul khrims kyi mtshon las myi 'da' ba dang/blo rgya mtsho dang sa ltar che ba'i mchog tu gyurd pa/sa dang chu dang me dang rlung mnyam ba'i sems su gyurd pa/ri rab ltar brtan zhing myi g.yo' la skyod par myi nus pa'i yid
6b9(1): dang ldan ba/rjes su chags pa dang khong khro ba myed pa dang/nam kha'i ngos ltar dri ma myed cing yangs la 'dri ba myedapar rgya che ba'i blo dang ldan ba/lhag pa'i bsam pa shind tu yongs su dag pa/sbyin ba legs bar
6b10(1): byin ba/sngon sbyor ba legs par byin ba/sngon sbyor ba leg par byas pa/lhag pa'i bya ba legs par byas pa/bden ba'i rgyand kyis legs par brgyand pa/dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad yongs su btsal
7a1(1): //pa gos par bya ba bgos pas/dge ba'i rtsa ba des par byas pa/bskald pa grangs myed pa bdun du dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad yang dag par bsgrubs pa/sbyin pa rnam bdun byin ba/bsod nams bya ba'i
7a2(1): dngos po lnga la shind tu brtend pa/legs par spyad pa lus kyi rnam gsum/ngag gi rnam pa bzhi yid kyi rnam gsum spyad par gyurd pa/dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam brtend pa dang ldan ba/yang dag par sbyor ba yan
7a3(1): lag bzhi bcu dang ldan pa brtend par gyurd pa//yang dag pa'i smon lam yan lag bzhi bcu dang ldan ba smon lam btab pa/lhag pa'i bsam pa yan lag bzhi bcu dang ldang ba la zhugs pa/yang dag pa'i
7a4(1): rnam par thard pa'i yan lag bzhi bcu dang dang ldan bas yongs su rdzogs par byas pa dang/yang dag pa'i mos pa yan lag bzhi bcu dag ldan ba drang por byas pa/sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag bcu'i rjes su rab
7a5(1): tu byung ba/sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrag brgya stong lnga bcu rtsa lnga la sbyin ba phul bar gyurd pa/rang sangs rgyas bye ba brgya phrag bye bzhi la lhag par bya ba byas pa/sems can tshad myed pa grangs myed pa dag
7a6(1): mtho' ris dang thard pa'i lam btsud par gyurd pa bla na myed pa ya/nga dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar/'dod pa tshe gcig gis thogs pa 'di nas shi 'phos nas dga' 
7a7(1): ldan gyi gnas dam pa na gnas pa /lha'i bu dam pa tog kar/po zhes bya ba/lha'i tshogs rnams kyis 'di ni 'di nas shi 'phos na/sa/myi'i 'jig rten du skyes te/ring por myi thogs par bla na 
7a8(1): myed pa yang dag par rdzogs byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshad rgya bar 'gyur ro zhes der shind tu mchod pa /gzhal myed khang nas sum khri nyi stong rab tu gnas pa /bstegs bu dang ba gam dang/rta bas babs
7a9(1): dang/skar khung dang/gsil khang dang/khang brtsegs pa dang/khyams kyis legs par brgyand pa dang/gdugs dang/rgyal mtshan dang ba dan bsgreng ba dang/rin po che'i dril bu g.yer ka'i dra ba'i bla re 
7a10(1): bres pa/me tog man da ra ba dang/man da ra ba chen po 'chald par bkram ba/lha'i bu mo bye ba khrag khrig klu dbyangs len ba /me tog a ti mug ta dang/tsam pa ka dang/skya s+Shar dang/ko pi da ra dang/btang 
7b1(1): gzungs chen po dang/mya ngan tshang dang/n+ya ro ta dang /tin ka dang/a na dang/dong ka'i me tog dang/shing kyi sa ra dang/sa la dang/rin po che'i shing ljon pa chen pos brgyadad pa/gser gyi dra bas g.yongs pa/gzhi mnyam
7b2(1): zhing bkod pas mdzes par byas pa/me tog 'ong dang/ma li ka dang/snang ma'i me tog dang/par shi ka dang/ta ra ni dang/ba li dang/go ta ra ni dri rab tu zhim pa dang/da nus ka ri dang/lha'i snang ma'i me tog dang/ud 
7b3(1): pa la dang/pad ma dang/ku mu da dang/pad ma kar po dang/dri rab tu zhim pa'i me tog dang/bla re chen po phyogs phyogs su bres pa/bya mda' ba sbed dang/ne tso dang/ri skegs dang/khu byug dang/ngang pa dang/rma bya dang/
7b4(1): ngur pa dang/ku na la dang/ka la bing ka dang/shang shang de'u lasstsogs pa bya sna tshogs sgra snyan 'byind pa/lha bye khrag khrig brgya stong gis myiga mngon tu phyogs te/blta zhing bltad mor bya ba/chos rgya che ba 
7b5(1): chen po yang dag par brjod pas 'dod pa la dga' ba'i shugs kyi nyon mongs pa thams cad gcod pa/nga rgyal dang/rgyags pa dang/dregs pa dang/khro ba dang/thab pa dang/khong khro ba myed par 
7b6(1): gyurd pa/dga' ba dang/mgu ba dang/mchog tu dga' ba dang/dran ba rgya che zhing gsal ba skye ba'i gzhal yas khang chen de na gnasshing chos kyi yang dag pa'i gtam byung ba dang/bsil snyan dang/
7b7(1): klu dbyangs brgya khri bzhi stong ba'i sgra de dag la byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi las dge ba'i tshogs kyis yang dag par bskul ba'i tshigs su bcad ba 'di dag 'byung ngo//bsod nams rgya chen tshogs mnga' dran rtogs dang
7b8(1): blo gros mtha' yas shes rab 'od mdzad pa//mtshungs myed stobs mnga' sku thal rgya che ba//mar me mdzad kyis lung bstan dgongs par mdzod//rgya chen dri ma myed pa'i thugs mnga' ba//dri ma gsum spangs
7b9(1): rgyags dang skyon zhi ba//dge ba dri myed rnam par dag pa'i thugs//sngon chad ji ltar sbyin spyad dgongs par mdzod//zhi gnas tshul khrims rtul zhugs zad pa dang//'dul dang brtson 'grus bsam gtan
7b10(1): shes rab dang/bskald pa bye ba khrag khrig rnams spyad pa//spyod rigs cher 'byung dgongs mdzod dgongs par mdzod/sems can rnams la thugs rtse mdzad gyurd cing//sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrig
8a1(1): //khyod kyis mchod//dgongs mdzod dgongs par mdzod cig grags pa mtha' yas pa//de dus lags kyis btang snyoms ma mdzad cig//rga shi nyon mongs 'jigs mdzad rdul bral ba//lha dang klu dang
8a2(1): gnod sbyin dri za dang//lha myin dbang po dag kyang sdod lags kyis //'chi 'pho ba'i tshul mkhyend 'gro ba bstand par mdzod //skal ba stong du dga' ba spyad 'gyurd kyang//rgya mtsho chu yis ji bzhin myi ngos kyis
8a3(1): ngoms pa khyod kyis thugs dam dges pa ste//yun rings sku mnyen skye bo 'tshim par mdzod //smad pa myed pa'i grags pa mnga' ba khyod //chos la dges pas khyod dgyes 'dod dgyes //'on kyang dri ma myed
8a4(1): pa'i spyan mnga' ba//lhar bcas 'jig rten dag la thugs rtse mdzod//lha rnams bye ba khyod las chos pas //ngoms par gyurd pa ma lags 'on kyis kyang//dal dang myi ldan gyurd pa'i
8a5(1): don gsung du//gnas par gyurd pa dag la gzigs su gsol//dri ma myed pa spyan mnga' phyogs bcu'i//'jig rten dag gis sangs rgyas gzigs gyurd cig//de dag rnams
8a6(1): las chos gsan chos mchog de//'jig rten dag la rnam par dbye ba mdzod//dpal ldan khyod kyi bsod nams khyod kyis ni//dga' ldan pho brang shind tu mdzes kyis//'on kyang thugs rje'i thugs
8a7(1): dang ldan pas na//'dzam bu'i rgyal mtshan du ni char pa phob//brtul zhugs grub pa byang chub reg ces te//'dod pa'i khams las rab tu 'das pa'i //gzugs kyi khams na lha rnams gang 'khor
8a8(1): ba //du ma thams cad mngon par rab tu dga'//mgon po khyod kyis bdud kyi las rnams macom//khyod gzhan mu stegs can rnams phan mdzad de//khyod ni byang chub lag thil bzhag dang 'dra//
8a9(1): de dus lags kyis btang snyoms ma mdzad cig//nyon mongs me rab 'bar ba'i 'jig rten la//dpa' bo khyod kyis sprin ltar khyab mdzad de//bdud rtsi'i char pa mngon par rab tu phob//lha dang myi rnams
8a10(1): nyon mongs nye bar mdzod//khyod ni sman pa mkhas la mkhas pa ste//yun ring nad pa sman pas bor ba'i//sems can rnam thar gsum gyi sbyar sman gyis//mya ngan 'das pa'i bde la myur tu khod//
8b1(1): seng ge'i nga ro thos bar ma gyurd pas/wa rnams 'jigs pa myed par sgra sgrogs kyis//sangs rgyas seng ge'i sgra ni rab sgrags te//mu stegs can gzhan wa rnams bskrag par mdzod//sa'i dkyil na stobs dang brtson 'grus 
8b2(1): kyi//stobs ldan phyag na shes rab sgron ma snams//phyag thil dam pas sa la brdab mdzod de//'di rnams khyod kyis rab tu pham bar mdzod//gang dag khyod la lhung mdzad dbul ba'i 'jig rten skyong ba bzhi po bstod 
8b3(1): cing mchis//khyod ltar gang dag legs par ma gyurd pa'i//brgya byin tshangs pa khrag khrig stod cing mchi'//blo gros bzang po rigs ldan ba//gang la gnas pas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa stobs gyurd daM 
8b4(1): pa rigs skyes pa//rigs kyi nang na btsun pa'i rigs la gzigs//snod gyurd gang tu nor bu rin po che//bzhag nas dpal ldan bar gyurd pa bzhi po 'di//nor bu rin cen lta bur dri myed thugs//'dzam bu 
8b5(1): rgyal mtshan du ni char ba phob//klu dbyangs sgra las rnam pa 'di lta'i//tshigs su bcad pa mang po byung nas//'di ni dus lags matang snyoms ma mdzad ces//snying rje'i yid dang ldan pa skul bar 
8b6(1): byed////yang dag par spro bar byed pa'i le'u ste//gnyis pa'o////dge slong dag de ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 
8b7(1): chos kyi dus de lta bus yang dag par bskuld pa dang/gzhal myed khang chen po de nas byung ste/chos kyis mtho ba zhes bya ba'i khang bzang sa chen po gang tu byang chub sems dpa' 'dug cing dga' ldan gyi lha rnams kyis 
8b8(1): chos stond pa der/byang chub sems dpa' bzhugs so/bzhugs nas kyang/seng ge'i khri chos bzang ngo bo zhes bya ba la 'dug go//de nas lha'i bu gang dag byang chub sems dpa' dang spyod pa 'dra 
8b9(1): ba dag dang/lha'i bu de dag dang/bskal pa mnyam ba dang/theg pa mthun pa la zhugs pa de dag kyang/khang bzangs de nyid du bzhugs so//gang yang byang chub sems dpa' phyogs bcu 
9a1(1): / ࿒ /nas 'dus pa byang chub sems dpa' dang spyod pa 'dra ba dag dang/lha'i bu de dag dang/lha'i bu mo'i tshogs rnams gsald cing /lha phal rnams kyang gsald nas kyang/lhag pa'i bsam pa mthun 
9a2(1): ba'i 'khor can du byas te/'khor bye ba phrag drug bcu rtsa brgyad brgyad dang/khang bzangs der zhugs nas/so so ci rigs par rang rang gi se ge'i khri la 'khod par gyurd to//de slong dag de ltar byang chub sems dpa' lo bcu gnyis
9a3(1): na ma'i mngal du 'jug par 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i tshe/gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu dag 'dzam bu'i gling du lhags nas/lha'i kha dog myi snang bar byas te/bram ze'i cha byad du bsgyurd nas/bram ze rnams rig byed slob tu
9a4(1): 'dzud de/gang zhig rnam pa 'di lta bur mngal du zhugs par gyurd na/de ni skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum bcu rtsa gnyis dang/ldan bar gyurd de dag dang ldan na/rnam pa gnyis su 'gyur gyi/gsum du 'gyur ba myed de
9a5(1): de gal te khyim na gnas pa na ni/'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po mtha'a bzhir rnam par rgyal ba chos dang ldan pa/chos kyi rgyal po rin po che/sna bdun dang ldan bar 'gyur ro//de'i rin po che sna bdun ni 'di lta ste 
9a6(1): 'khor lo rin po che dang/glang po rin po che dang/rta rin po che dang/bud myed rin po che dang/nor bu rin po che dang/khyim bdag rin po/che dang/blon po rin po che dang bdun no//'khor los sgyur 
9a7(1): ba'i rgyal po 'khor lo rin po che rnam pa ji lta bu dang ldan par 'gyur zhe na/'di la rgyal po rgyal rigs spi bo nas dbang skur bas/lha'i 'khor lo/rin po che de mthong nas/'di snyam du sems te/gso sbyong gi
9a8(1): nyi ma tshes bco lnga la mgo bo khrus byas te/dbu gzungs su gnang zhing steng gi khyams na/bud myed kyi tshogs kyis kun nas bskord cing 'dug pa'i tshe/shar phyogs nas lha'i 'khor lo rin po che rtsibs stong yod pa mu
9a9(1): khyud ldan pa/lte ba dang bcas pa/thams cad gser las grub pa/'gar bas ma byas pa/'phang du shing ta la bdun tsam nas byung ste/rgyal po Irgyal rigs spi bo nas dbad bskur bas/lha'i 'khor lo
9a10(1): rin po che de mthong nas 'di snyam du sems te/bdag gis thos pa rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo nas dbang bskurd pa/gso sbyong gi nyi ma'i tshes bco lnga mgo bo khrus byas te/dngu gzungs su gnas shing steng gi khyams na/
9b1(1): bud myed kyi tshogs kyis kun nas bskord cing 'dug pa'i tshe/shar phyogs nas lha'i 'khor lo rin po che 'byung bar gyurd/de ni 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal por gyurd to zhes thos na/bdag gi drung du lha'i 'khor lo rin po che ngos la 
9b2(1): las na/bdag kyang 'khor los sgyur ba yin du nges so/snyam ste de nas rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo nas dbang skur ba de/bla gos phrag pa gcig tu bzangad nas /pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa kha btsugs te/lag pa 
9b3(1): g.yas pas lha'i 'khor lo rin po chede bskor zhing 'di skad btsun ba/lha'i 'khor lo rin po che chos kyis bskord cing/chos ma yin pas ni ma yin ba zhes bya ba'i tshig smras pa dang/de nas lha'i 'khor lo rin pi che de rgyal po rgyal
9b4(1): rigs spyi bo nas dbang bskurd pas/rdzu 'phrul gyis nam mkha' las shar phyogs su legs par 'gro ste/rgyal po 'khor los sgyur ba yang/dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi pa dang lhan cig tu rjes su 'gro'/sa'i 
9b5(1): phyogs gang tu lha'i 'khor lo rin po ched de gnas par 'gyur te/rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo nas dpang skur ba/de yang dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi dang lhan cig tu gnas par 'gyur //de nas shar phyog+sa
9b6(1): kyi khams kyi rgyal po gang yin ba de dag/dngul gyi snod ni gser//phyes bkang ste thogs/gser gyi snod ni dngul gsil ma/sa bkang ste thogs nas/'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po su
9b7(1): zhig 'di skad du/lha 'dir legs pa gshegs so/lha tshur gshegs su gsol//lha'i chab srid ni shind tu rgyas shing 'byord la bde ste lo legs shing nyams bgyes la/skye bo mang po dang myis
9b8(1): gang ba lags na/lha nyid kyi yul du byon na bzhugs su gsol/de skad ces smras pa dang/rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo nas dbang skurd pas/khams kyi rgyal po de dag la 'di skad du/khyed rang rang gis rgyal srid 
9b9(1): dag chos kyis gyis shig/tshig kyal ba ma yin no//khyed srog chags ma gsod cig/ma byin par ma len cig/'dod pa log par g.yem pa ma byed cig/brdzun tu ma smra cig/phra ma ma zer cig/ngag
9b10(1): rdzu ba ma smra cig/tshig kyal ba ma smra cig/brnab sems can du ma gyurd cig/gnod sems can du ma gyurd ci ga/log par lta bar ma gyurd cig/srog gcod pa la byams par sa gyurd cig/log par lta ba
10a1(1): /can gyi bar la byams bar ma gyurd cig/nga'i yul du chos ma yin pa 'byung nga re/chos ma yin bar spyod pa la byams par ma gyurd cig ces bsgo nas/rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo nas dbang bskurd pas
10a2(1): ka/de ltar shar phyogs su rnam par rgyal bar byed do/shar phyogs su rgyal bar byas nas/shar phyogs kyi rgya mtsho la 'gro'o//shar phyogs kyi rgya mtsho la song nas/shar phyogs kyi rgya 'tsho las brgal lo/rgal nas kyang 
10a3(1): lho phyogs nam mkha' las rdzu 'phrul gyis legs par 'gro ste/'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po yang dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi dang lhan cig tu 'gro'o//snga ma bzhin du lho phyogs su rnam par rgyal par byed do/lho
10a4(1): phyogs su ji lta ba de bzhin du/nub phyogs dang/byang phyogs su rnam par rgyal ba byed do/byang phyogs su rnam pa rgyal bar byas nas/byang phyogs kyi rgya mtsho la 'gro 'o/byang phyogs kyi rgya mtshor song nas/byang
10a5(1): phyogs kyi rgya mtsho las rgald to/brgald nas kyang /nam mkha' las rdzu 'phrul gyisargyal po'i pho brang du legs par 'ongs nas/btsun mo'i 'khor kyi sgo'i steng du /nyams pa myed par 
10a6(1): gnas te/rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo nas dbang bskur ba ni/'khor lo rin po che rnam pa de lta bu dang ldan pa yin no//'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po glang po rin po che rnam pa ji lta bu dang ldan pa
10a7(1): yin zhe na/'di la rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo na dbang bskur ba'i glang po rin po che snga ma bzhin du 'byung ste/thams cad thal kar du gyurd pa yan lag bdun shind tu gnas pa/gtsug gser gyis brgyand
10a8(1): pa/gser gyi rgyal mtshan dang ldan ba/gser gyi rgyand kyis brgyand ba/gser gyi dra bas g.yogs pa/rdzu 'phrul dang ldan ba/nam kha las 'gro ba rnam par 'phrul pa'i chos can te/'di ltar glang po 
10a9(1): che'i rgyal po byang chub ces bya'o/nam rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bonas dbang bskur ba/glang po rin po rin po che de brtag par 'dod par gyurd pa de'i tshe/nyi ma shar kar glang po che de la zhon te sa chen po 'di rgya tsho'i mtha' skor ba dang bcas pa
10a10(1): rgya mtsho'i mur thug pa dang bcas pa kun bskor te/rgyal po'i bro brang du 'ongs nas sus kyi dga' ba myong bar byed do/'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po ni glang po rin po che rnam pa yin no//'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal bo rta rin po che
10b1(1): rnam pa ji lta bu dang ldan pa yin zhe na/'di la rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo nas dbang bskur ba'i rta rin po che snga ma bzhin du 'byung ste/thams cad sngon por gyurd pa/mgo nag pa/lcang lo rnga ma phyang bar gyurd ba/zhon na gus par 
10b2(1): byed pa/gser gyi rgyal mtshan dang bcas pa/gser gyi rgyand dang ldan ba/gser gyi dra bas g.yogs pa/rdzu 'phrul dang ldan ba/nam mkha' la 'gro ba/rnam par 'phrul ba'i chos can te/'di ltar rta'i rgyal po sprin
10b3(1): gyi shugs can zhes bya'o/nam rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo nis dbang sgyur ba/rta rin po che brtag par 'dod par gyurd pa de'i tshe nyi ma shar kar rta rin po che de la zhon nas sa chen po 'di rgya mtsho'i mtha'i skor dang bcas pa/rgya mtsho chen
10b4(1): bor mur thugs dang bcas pa kun tu bskor te/phyir rgyal po'i pho brang 'khor du/khye'u sus kyi bde ba myong bar byed de/'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po ni rta rin po che rnam pa de lta bu dang ldan pa yin no/'khor los sgyur ba'i
10b5(1): rgyal po ni nor bu rin po che rnam ji lta bu dang ldan ba yin zhe na/'di la rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bonas dbang skur ba'i nor bu rin po che snga ma bzhin du 'byung ba ste/thams cad du sngo ba/be du r+ya'i rang bzhin
10b6(1): zung brgyad du 'dug pa/shind tu sbyangs pa 'byung ste/nor bu rin po che de'i 'od kyis mtshun mo'i 'khor thams cad kyi snang bar khyab par 'gyur ro/nam rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi po nas dbang skur 
10b7(1): ba/nor bu rin po che de rtag par 'dod par gyurd ba//de'i tshe mtshan mo nam gyi gung la mun pa mun nag gi nang du/nor bu rin po che dergyal mtshan gyi rtse mo la btags te/skyed mos tshal gyi sa bzang po
10b8(1): blta ba'i phyir song na/nor bu rin po de de'i 'od kyis thams cad dang ldan ba'i dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi pa thams cad dpag tshad 'khor ra 'khor du (ma)tu snang bas gsal bar gyurd to//nor bu rin po che de
10b9(1): de'i 'khor na gnas pa'i spyi de dag kyang snang ba des gsal bar gyurd pa dang/gcig gis gcig mthong/gcig gis gcig shes/gcig gis gcig ji skad smra ste/bzhin bzangs dag longs/las
10b10(1): kyi mtha' dag byos shig/chod dus dag khrom nyi ma shar te nyin mor shes so zer te/rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo nas dbang bskur te/nor bu rin po che rnam pa de lta bu dang ldan ba yin no/rgyal po rgyal rigs sbyi bo nas dbang
11a1(1): //skurd pa/bud myed rin po'i rnam pa ji lta bu dang ldan pa gang zhe na/'di la rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo nas dbang bskur ba'i bud myed rin po che snga ma bzhin du 'byung ste/tshul dang mthun ba/rgyal rigs su skyes ba/
11a2(1): ha cang yang myi ring ba/ha cang yang myi thung ba/ha cang yang myi sbom pa/ha cang yang myi skyem ba/ha cang yang myi kar ba/ha cang yang spyi gnas pa/gzugs mdzes pa/tshul du shis pa/blta na sdug pa/kha dog
11a3(1): bzang po/rgyas mchog dang ldan pa/de'i ba spu'i khu du thams cad nas/tsan dan gyi(da) 'byung ba/kha nas sud pa la'i dad ldang ba/ka tsa lin da ka'i gos ltar reg na bde ba/de grang ba'i dus na ni lus la reg na dro ba/
11a4(1): dro ba'i dus na lus la reg na bsil ba ste/de'i 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po ma gtogs par gzhan la/yid kyis kyang 'dod chags par myi byed na lus kyis lta ji smos te/'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal bo ni bud
11a5(1): myed rin po che rnam pa de lta bu dang ldan ba yin no/'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal pokhyim bdag rin po che rnam pa ji lta bu dang ldan pa yin zhe na/'di la rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo nas dbang skur ba'i khyim
11a6(1): bdag rin po che snga ma bzhin du 'byung ste/mkhas pa gsal ba/yid gzhungs pa lha'i dmyig dang ldan par 'byung ngo//de'i myig de nas dpag tshad 'khor yug tu bdago(da) dang bcas pa'i nor gyi
11a7(1): rter dag kyang mthong ste/de bdag po myed pa'i gter gyi nor gyi gter gyi nor gang yin pa gang dag gis 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal pa'i nor gyis dgos pa myed de/'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal (po) ni khyim bdag rin po che
11a8(1): rnam pa de lta bu dang ldanab yin no//'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal pe'i blon po rin po che rnam pa ji lta ba dang ldan ba yin zhe na/'di las 'khor los sgyur ba'i blon po rin po che snga ma bzhin (de)du 'byung ste/mkhas shing
11a9(1): gsal la yid gzhungs pa'o/de 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po des bsams pa tsam gyis sgo ba'i bya ba'i dpung rnams sko'o//'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal( )po ni blon po rin po che rnam (de)de lta bu dang ldan ba yin te/
11a10(1): 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal bo ni rin po che rnam pa de dag dang ldan pa yin no//de la bu stong byung ste/dpa' ba rtul phod pa/yan lag gi mchog gi gzugs dang ldan ba/pha rold kyi sde rab tu 'joms pa rag
11b1(1): stag ste/des rgya mtsho la thug pa'i sa chen po ma lus par gnod par sngo ba chen po myed cing /'tshe ba myed pa 'di nyid chad pa myed cing mtshon gyis 'da' ba myed la chos kyis legs par phab ste gnas so//gal te khyim nas khyim
11b2(1): myed par rab tu byung na/sangs rgyas su 'gyur te/'dod pa'i 'dod chags spangs pa 'drend pa gzhan gyi drid myi 'jog pa/lha dang myi rnams kyi stond par 'gyur ro zhes/rigs 'don tu 'dzud do//de bzhin du lha'i
11b3(1): bu gzhan kyang 'dzam bu'i gling du lhags shing rang sangs rgyas dag la skul ma 'debs te/'phags ba dag da ste le bcu gnyis na/byang chub sems dpa' yum gyi lhums su 'jug gis/sangs rgyas kyi zhing der 'or cig zer 
11b4(1): ro/dge slong dag de'i tshe rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer chen po na/'jug ma sgyur zhes bya ba'i ri la/rang sangs rgyas glang po che zhes bya ba 'dug pas/sgra de thos pas/rdo'i steng du 'jim pa la bya ba bzhin
11b5(1): du 'dug ste/nam kha la shing ta la bdun srid du gyen tu 'phags so/gyend tu 'phags nas kyang mye'i khams la snyomspar zhugs te/sgron ma bzhin du mya ngan las 'das so//
11b6(1): de'i khrigs pa dang bad kan dang chu rgyu ba dang/rus pa dang/sha dang/khrag tu rgyu ba gang yin ba de thams cad ni mes yongs su zada par 'gyur te/ring srel 'ba' shig sa la lhags nas /rkang 
11b7(1): rjen de dag ni da dung yang drang srong gi rjes shes grag ste yod do/dge slong dag de'i tshe yul ba ra na se'i ri dgas kyi nags drang srong ltung ba na/sangs rgyas lnga brgya 'khor ba de dag gis kyang sgra de thos nas /nam kha' la 
11b8(1): shing ta la bdun srid du gyon tu 'phags nas/me'i khams la snyoms par zhugs te/sgron ma bzhIn du yongs su mya ngan las 'das so/de dag gi khrigs pa dang/bad kan dang/chu rgyus dang/rus pa dang sha dang/
11b9(1): khrag tu 'gyur ba deng yin ba de thams cad mi mes yongs su zad par 'gyur te/ring bsrel 'ba' shig sa la lhas so//de ltar 'dir drang srong rnams ltung ngo/zhes de de phyin chad drang srong ltung ba zhes bya ba yang /
11b10(1): drang srong ltung ba zhes mying Idu gyurd to//dge slong dag myi 'jigs pa byin ba'i ri dgas dag kyang de na gnas pas/de phan chad ri dgas kyi nags shes bya ba yang /ri dgas kyi nags shes mying du gyurd to//
12a1(1): //dge slong dag de ltar byang chub sems dpa' dga' ldan gyi gnas dam pa na 'dug pa ni/rnam par lta ba chen po bzhi la rnam par lta ste/bzhi gang zhe na 'di lta ste/dus rnam par lta ba dang/gling rnam par
12a2(1): ka lta ba dang/yul rnam par lta ba dang/rigs rnam par lta ba dang/dge slong dag ci'i phyir byang chub sems dpa'i dus rnam par lta ba la rnam par lta zhe na/byang chub sems dpa' ni thog ma'i dus na/sems can 
12a3(1): 'du ba'i tshe/ma'i mngal du myi 'jug gi/gang gis 'jig rten gsal ba kun nas gnas par gyurd te/skye ba snang bar gyurd/dga' ba snang bar gyurd /na ba snang bar gyurd/'chi ba snang bar gyurd pa de'i
12a4(1): tshe byang chub sems dpa' ma'i mngal du 'jug go//dge slong dag ci'i phyir byung chub sems dpa' gling rnam par lta ba rnam par lta shes na/byang chub sems dpa'i ni mtha' 'khob kyi gling du myi skye/shar kyi
12a5(1): lus phags bor myi skye/nub kyi ba lang spyod du myi skye/byang gi sgra myi snyan du myi skye la/'dzam bu'i gling nyid du skye'o/dge slong dag ci'i phyir byang chub sem dpa' ni thar khob yul nang na/
12a6(1): myi rnams dbang po brtul zhing blun la/lug ltar blun ba'i rang bzhin can legs par brtan pa dang/nye bar bstand pa'i don shes par bya ba'i skal ba myed pa dag yod pa hU skye'o/byang chub
12a7(1): sems dpa'i ni yul gyi dbus nyid du skye'o//dge slong dag ci'phyir byang chub sems dpa'i rigs rnams lta ba la rnam par lta zhe na/byang chub sems dpa' ni rigs rdol pa'i rigs sam/
12a8(1): dmyig mkhan gyi rigs sam/shing rta mkhan gyi rigs sam/g.yung rigs su myi skye'i/bram ze'i rigs sam/rgyal rigs kho na ste/rigs gnyis su skye'o/de la gang gi tshe bram ze'i rigs btsun
12a9(1): bar bya ba de'i tshe bram ze'i rigs su skye'o/gang gi tshe rgyal rigs btsun bar bya ba de'i tshe rgyal rigs su skye ste/dge slong dag da ltar ni rgyal rigs btsun bar bya pa de'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' rgyal rigs su skye ste
12a10(1): dge slong dag don gyi dbang de la brtend nas/byang chub sems dpa' dga' ldan gyi gnas na gnas pa/rnam par lta ba chen po bzhi la rnam par lta ste/de ltar bltas nas cad myi zer bar gyurd to /dge slong dag
12b1(1): de ltar lha'i bu de dag dang/byang chub sems dpa' de dag gcig la gcig la 'di skad du /byang chub sems dpa'i rigs chen po ni ji lta bu zhig skye bar gyurd/yum ni ji lta bu zhig gi lhums su 'jug par 'gyur ro zhes
12b2(1): 'dri'o//de la la na re/yul ma ga tan lus 'phags kyi rigs 'byord cing rgyas la /lo legs pa dang/bde ba dang ldan ba yin te/de ni byang chub sems dpa' yum gyi lhums su 'jug pa'i gnas su rung
12b3(1): ngo zhes zer ro/la la ne re de ni tshul dang mthun ba ma yin te/de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar de ni ma'i rgyud kyis dag pa yang ma yin la/pha'i rgyud kyis dag pa yang mayin la/bsod nams chu ngus 'byung gi/
12b4(1): bsod nams chen pos ngos mngon bar 'byung ba yang ma yin zhing/rgoda la myi brtan te/myi gnas pa ma yin la/yul de'i nye rab 'khor gsega mas gang ba skyed mos 'tshal dang/mtshe'u dang lu ma dag
12b5(1): kyang myi mang la ri brags kyi grong ltar mtha' khob kyi gnas su gyurd pa ste/de'i phyir tshul dang mthun ba ma yi no zhes zer ro/la la/na re ko sa la'i rigs 'di 'khor mang zhing gzhon ba mang la/
12b6(1): nor che ba ste/de ni byang chub sems pa' yum gyi lhums su 'juga pa'i gnas su rung ngo zhes zer ro//la la na re yang tshul dang 'thun pa ma yin te/de ci'i phyid zhe na/'di ltar ko sa la'i rigs
12b7(1): ni/rigs ngan gyi rgyud nas shi 'phos nas/skyes pa yin te/pha dang mas dag pa yang ma yin la/dam pa la mos pa ste/rigs mtho' bar yang myi bya/nor dang rin po che'i rteng yang tshad myed par phun sum tshogs
12b8(1): ba ma yin te/de'i phyir de ni tshul dang mthun ba ma yin no zhes zer ro/ O /la la na re rgyal po pa sa'i rigs 'di 'byord cing rgyas la lo legs pa dang/bde ba dang ldan ba yin te/de ni byang chub seIIdpa'
12b9(1): yum gyi yum gyi lhums su 'jug pa'i gnas su rung ngo zhe zer ro/la la na re de yang de'i tshul dang mthun ba ma yid te/de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar rgyal po pad ma'i rigs ni phal pa ste/bltum la gzi brjid 
12b10(1): 'bar ba yang ma yin zhing/skyes pa gzhan las skye ba grub pa pha ma nyid kyi gzi brjid kyi las kyis grub pa ma yin la/de'i rgyal po yang chad pa smra ste/de'i phyir de yang tshul dang mthun ba ma yi no zhes zer ro/la la
13a1(1): //na re grong khyer chen po yangs pa chen 'di ni 'byord cing rgyas pa /lo legs pa dang/bde ba dang ldan ba yin te nyams dga' skye bo mang po dang/myis gang ba /stegs bu dang ba gam dang/rta bas dang/skar 
13a2(1): ka/khung dang/bsil khang dang/khang pa brtsegs pa dang/khyams dag gis legs par brgyand ba ste/me tog gi ra ba dang nags tshal gyi phreng ba me tog kun tu rgyas pa dang ldan ba yin la/lha'i pho brang 'dra ba yin te/de ni byang 
13a3(1): chub sems dpa' yum gyi lhums su 'jug pa'i gnas su rung ngo zhes zer ro//la la na re de yang tshul dang mthun ba ma yin no/de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar de dag la ni phan tshun du rigs par smra ba myed do/chos spyod pa myedo
13a4(1): gong ma dang/bar ma dang/rgan pa dang/gtso bo dag la yang rim gror bya ba myed cing/re re zhing yang 'di snyam du bdag ni rgyal po'o/bdag ni rgyal po'o snyam du sems pas/slob mar khas lend pa myed cing chos khas 
13a5(1): lend pa myed pa ste/de'i phyir de yang tshul dang mthun pa ma yin no//zhes zer ro/la la na re grong khyer 'phags rgyal na/bar snang gi rigs 'di dbung che zhing bzhon pa mang ba/pha rold kyi g.yul 
13a6(1): ngor rgyal ba thob pa yin te/de ni byang chub sems dpa' yu gyi lhums su 'jug pa'i gsanas su rung ngo zhes zer ro //la la ni re de yang tshul dang mthunab ma yin te/de ci'i phyir zhe na/
13a7(1): 'di ltar de dag ni khro zhing gtum la brlang zhing zu lums can rgod pa ste/las yod pa 'di ma yin pas/de'i phyir de yang byang chub sems dpa' de lhums su 'jug pa'i gnas kyi 'os ma yin no zhes zer ro/
13a8(1): la la na re grong khyer bcos rlag 'di ni 'byord cing rgyas la lo legs pa dang bde ba dang ldan ba/skye bo mang po dang myis kun tu gang ba yin ba/rgyal po lag bzangs sde dpa' ba'i rgyal po'i pho brang de ni byang chub 
13a9(1): sems dpa' yum gyi lhums su 'jug pa'i gnasasu rung ngo zhes zer ro/la la na re de yang tshul dang mthun pa ma yin te/de ci'i phyir zha na/'di ltar rgyal po de ni log par lta ba'i rigs kyi rgyud du skyes pa ste/
13a10(1): myi rgod dang 'dra ba'i rgyal po yin bas byang chub sems dpa' srid pa ni lo bar lta ba'i rigs su skye bar myi rigs te/de'i phyir de yang tshul dang mthun bar ma yin no zhes zer ro //la la na re grong khyer ha sdin pu ra 
13b1(1): 'di'i rgyal bo ni skya bseng gi bu'i rigs rgyud las skyes pa /dpa' zhing rtul phod dang ldan ba /yan lag mchog gi gzugs phun sum tshogs pa /gzhan gyi sde 'jom ba ste /rigs de ni byang chub sems dpa' de ni 
13b2(1): lhums su 'jug pa'i gnas su 'os pa yin no zhes zer ro/la la na re de yang tshul dang mthun ba ma yin no zhes zer te/de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar skya bseng gi bu'i rigs su skye ba dag gis ni rigs kyi rgyud rnam 
13b3(1): par 'grugs par byas te/yul dngor brtan ba la ni chos kyi bu zhes zer /sde 'jigs su rung ba ni rlung gi bu zhes zer/srid grub pa la ni dbang po'i bu zhes zer /rigs byed dang lhar bcas pa la ni tha skar gyi
13b4(1): bu zhes zer te/de'i phyir rigs de yang byang chub sems dpa'de lhums su 'jug pa'i gnasu yin 'os pa yin no zhes zer ro/la la na re grong khyer bcos glag 'di ni shind tu yang nyams dga' zhing phan tshun
13b5(1): dgyed kyi rgyal po bzang po bshes gnyen bzhugs pa'i sa yang yin la/rgyal po de yang glang po dang rta dang shing rta dang/dpung bu chung dang/dpung gi tshogs mang po ldan ba gser dang/
13b6(1): dngul dang/nor bu dang/mu tig dang/be du r+ya dang/dung dang/man shel dang/byi du dang/sa le sbram dang/nor dang/yobyad shind tu mang ba dang/rgyal po dang blon po thams cad kyis zild
13b7(1): kyis myi gnond pa'i mthu' rtsal dang ldan ba /mdza' bo dang ldan pa chos dang ldan pa ste/rigs de ni byang chub sems dpa' de ni yum gyi lhums su 'jug pa'i gnas su rung ngo zhes zer ro/la la na re de yang tshul de dang
13b8(1): mthun ba ma yin te/de ci'i phyir zhe na/rgyal po bzang po'i bshes gnyen yon tan de lta bu dang ldan ba yod mod kyi/de ni shind tu rgas pas bu skyed pa'i mthu yang myed la bu yang mang bas/de'i phyir rigs de/byang chub 
13b9(1): sems dpa' de yum lhums su 'jug pa'i gnasakyi 'os ma yin no zhes zer ro/de ltar byang chub sems dpa' de dag dang lha de dag gis 'dzam bu'i gling gi yul chen po bcu drug thams cad na/rgyal po'i rigs thon po gang ji snyed
13b10(1): yod pa de dag thams cad la bltas pas/thams cad kyang nyes pa dang bcas par mthong ste/de dag de ltar sems pa'i yid la byed pa'i tshe/lha'i bu ye shes tog gi rgyal mtshan zhes bya ba/byang chub las phyir myi ldog pa'i theg pa 
14a1(1): //chen po 'dis nyes par gyurd pa zhig yod pa des/byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor lha'i 'khor chen po de dag la 'di skad ces smras so//grogs po dag tshur shog/byang chub sems dpa' de nyid kyi drung du dong ste/rigs
14a2(1): karin po che yon tan ji lta bur phun sum tshogs pa zhig tu srid pa tha ma pa'i byang chub sems dpa' skye bar gyurd pa dri' zhes smras pa dang/legs so zhes te/de dag thams cad kyang thal mo sbyard nas/byang chub sems
14a3(1): dpa'i drung du phyind te dris pa/skyes bu dam pa'i rigs rin po che yon tan phun sum tshogs pa ji lta bu zhig tu byang chub sems dpa' srid pa tha ma pa skye bar 'gyur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa'as byang chub sems
14a4(1): dpa'i tshogs chen po dang/lha'i tshogs chen po de dag la rnam par ltas te/'di skad ces smras so//grog po dag byang chub sems dpa' 'dri da pa tha ma pa skye bar 'grur ro//de nas byang chub 
14a5(1): sems dpa'as byang chub kyi sems dpa' yin te drug cu rtsa bzhi gang zhe na 'di lta/ste/rigs de ni btsun pa yin/rigs de ni kun kyis mngon par shes pa yin/rigs de ni phran tshegs myed cing gnod pa byed pa
14a6(1): myed pa yin/rigs de ni rgyud phun sum tshogs pa yin /rigs de ni cho 'brang phun sum tshogs pa yin/rigs de ni skyes bu zung phun sum tshogs pa yinarigs de ni sngon gyi skyes bu zung phun su
14a7(1): tshogs pa yin/rigs de ni btsun ba'i skyes bu phun sum tshogs pa yin//rigs de ni kun kyis mdon bar shes pa'i skyes bu zung phun sum tshog pa yin/rigs de ni dbang che bar grags pa'i skyes bu zung phun 
14a8(1): sum tshogs pa yin/rigs da ni skye ba mang ba yin/rigs de ni bu myed pa yin/rigs de ni 'jigs pa myed pa yin/rigs de ni dman pa myed pa yin/rigs deni brgam pa myed pa yin/rigs da ni tshul khrims dang ldan pa yin/rigs
14a9(1): de ni shes rab dang ldan pa yin/rigs de ni blon pos lta zhing longs spyod la spos pa yin/rigs de ni gzo don yod pas bshags shing longs spyod la spyod pa yin/rigs de ni mdza' ba brtan pa yin/rigs de ni dud 'gro'i
14a10(1): skye gnas su skyes pa'i srog chags kyi srog la lta ba yin/rigs de ni byas pa gzo' ba yin/rigs de ni rtul zhugs shes pa yin/rigs de ni 'dun pas myi mgro ba yin/rigs de ni zhe sdang gis myi 'gro ba
14b1(1): yin/rigs de ni gti mug gis mgro ba yin/rigs de ni 'jigs par myi 'gro ba yin/rigs de ni+i kha na ma tho bas 'jigs pa yin/rigs de ni gti mu la myi gnas pa yin/rigs de ni lag swangs che ba yin/rigs de ni
14b2(1): bya ba la mos pa yin/rigs de nigatong ba la mos pa yin/'jigs de ni sbyin ba la mos pa yin/rig de ni zho shas che ba yin/rigs te ni brtsal brtan ba yin/rigs de ni mthu' rtsal dang ldan ba yin/rigs de ni rtsal
14b3(1): mchog dang ldan ba yin/rig de ni drang srong mchod pa yin/rigs de ni lha mchod pa yin/rigs te ni mchod rten mchod pa yin/rigs re ni chu na brjed pa yin/rigs de ni 'khon du 'dzind pa myed pa yin/rigs de ni phyogs
14b4(1): bcur sgrags pa yin/rigs de na 'khor chen po dang ldan ba yin/rigsde ni 'khor phyi phyed pa dang ldan ba yin/rigasade ni bla na myed pa'i 'khor dang ldan ba yin/rigs/de ni rigs de'i nang na gtso bor byas pa yin/rigs 'di ni
14b5(1): rigs la dbang thob ba yin/rigs de ni dpar cher grags pa yin/rigs de ni phar 'dzind pa yin/rigs de mi mar 'dzind pa yin/rig deni dge sbyong du 'dzind pa yin/rigs/de ni bram zer 'dzind pa yin/
14b6(1): rigs de ni nor dang 'bru'i bang mchod mang ba yin/rigs de ni bad+yigdang/gser dang/nor bu dang/mu tig dang/be du rgya dang/dung dang/man shel dang/bi du dang/sa le sbram dang/dngul dang/rdza sa dang
14b7(1): yo byad mang ba dang/rigs de ni glang po che dang/rta dang/rnga mo dang/ba lang dang/lug shind tu mang ba yin/rigs deni bran pho dang/bran mo dang/las byed zho shas 'tsho ba shind tu mang ba yin/rigs de na
14b8(1): thub bar dka' ba yin/rigs de ni don thams cad grub pa yin/rigs de ni 'khor los sgyur ba'i rigs kyi rgyud du skye ba yin/rigs de ni sngon gyi dge ba'i rtsa ba'i grogs kyis skyed pa yin/rigs de ni byang chub sems dpa'i rigs
14b9(1): su rigs btsun par skye ba yin/rigs deni 'jig rten lha dang bcas pa/bdud dang bcas la/tshangs pa dang bcas la/dge sbyong dang bram ze'i skye dgu dang bcas pa'i nang na/rigs las smos pa'i skyon kyi kha na ma tho ba thams cad dang
14b10(1): bral ba ste/grogs po dag gang tu byang chub sems dpa' srid pa tha ma pa 'byung bar gyurd pa'i rigs de ni rnam pa drug bcu rtsa bzhi po de dag dang ldan ba yin no//grogs po dag srid pa tha ma pa'i byang chub sems dpa' bud myed gang gi
15a1(1): //dpal du 'jug par gyurd pa'i bud myed de ni yon tan rnam pa sum bcu rtsa gnyis dang ldan ba'i yin te/sum bcu rtsa gnyis gang zhe na/'di lta ste/byang chub sems dpa' srid pa tha ma ni kun kyis mngon bar shes
15a2(1): pa'i bud kyi dpal du 'jug go/kun kyis mngon bar rig pa dang/spyod pa la glags myi thod ba dang/rigs phun sum tshogs pa dang/cho 'brang phun sum tshogs pa dang /gzugs phun sum tshogs pa dang/mying phun sum
15a3(1): tshogs pa dang/chu zheng phun sum tshogs pa/sngan chad ma btsas pa dang/tshul khrims phun sum tshogs pa dang gtong ba phun sum tshogs pa dang//bzhin 'dzum ba dang/'thun bar 'dzind ba dang/gsal ba/dul ba dang
15a4(1): 'jigs pa myed pa dang/mang du thos pa dang/mkhas pa dang/sgyu myed pa dang/g.yo' ba myed pa dang/myi khro ba dang/phro dag myed pa dang/ser sna myed pa dang/myi rgod pa dang/myi g.yeng ba dang/ca co myed
15a5(1): ba dang/bzod cing des pa dang ldan ba dang/ngo tsha shes shing/khrel yod pa dang/'dod chags dang/zhe sdang dang/gti mug gi chas chung ba dang/bud myed kyi skyon dang bral ba dang/khyim thab rtul zhugs
15a6(1): dang ldan ba dang/yon tan thams cad phun sum tshogs ba'i bud myed kyi mngal du byang chub sems dpa' srid pa tha ma la 'jug go/grogs po dag byang chub sems dpa' sring ba tha ma la bud myed gang gi mngal
15a7(1): du 'jug pa'i bud myed de ni yon gyi rnam pa sum cu rtsa gnyis po de dag dang ldan ba yin no//grogs po dag byang chub sem dpa' ni zla ba mar gyi ngo la ma'i mngal du myi 'jug gi/byang chub sems dpa' srid ba tha ma
15a8(1): ba'i zla ba yang gyi ngo'i tshes bco lnga'i zla ba nya ste rgyas pa la skar ma rgyal dang ldan ba'i tshe/ma gsong sbyong blangs pa'i mngal du 'jug go//de nas byang chub sems dpa' de dag dang lha'i bu de dag gis byang chub sems
15a9(1): dpa' las rigs yongs su dag pa dang/ma yongs su dag pa'i rnam pa de lta bu de dag thos nas 'di snyam du sems te/skyes bu dam 'dis rnam ji snyed du bstand pa de lta bu'i yon tan dang ldan ba'i rigs de gang
15a10(1): zhig yod snyam du sems so/de ltar sems shing yid la byed ba la zhugs pa de dag 'di snyam ste/shag kya'i grong 'di 'byord cing rgyas la bde zhing lo legs pa dang ldan ba ste/nyams dga' zhing skye bo mang bo dang/
15b1(1): myis gang ba yin la /rgyal po zas gtsang ma yang ma'i rgyud kyis dag pa/pha'i rgyud kyis dag pa/chung ma dag pa dang ldan ba/las kyi mtha' la nyon mongs ba myed pa /dpe' byad bzang po/shind tu mdzangs pa /bsod nams
15b2(1): kyi gzi brjid dang ldan ba /grangs pa chen po'i rigs su skyes pa/'khor los sgyur ba'i rigs rgyud du skyes pa/nor dang rter dang rin po che dpag tu myed pa dang ldan ba /las yod par lta ba/sdig pa'i lta ba myed pa/
15b3(1): shag kya'i yul kun na yang rgyal po de gcig pur zad de/tshong dpon dang/khyim bdag dang/blon po dang/'khor gyi phyi thams cad kyis rim gror bya zhing mchod pa/'dzes pa dang lta na sdug pa/ha cang yang myi rgan ba/
15b4(1): ha cang yang myi gzhon zhing /gzugs mdzes la yon (na)thams cad dang ldan ba /bzo' shes pa/dus shes pa/bdag shes pa/chos shes pa/sems can shes pa/'jog rten shes pa/mtshan shes pa/chos
15b5(1): kyi rgyal po/chos kyis rjes su stond pa'o/gser skya'i gnas kyi sngon (po) grong khyer chen po de ni sems can dge ba'i rtsa ba skyed pa rnams kyi gnas te/gang dag der skyes par gyurd pa'i de dag kyang de dang skal
15b6(1): ba 'dra ba dag go//rgyal po zas cang ma de la lha mo sgyu 'phrul zhes bya ba/shag kya'i bdag po shind tu legs par rtogs pa/zhes bya ba'i bud myed yod pa 'ang phyug cing gzhon zhing dar la
15b7(1): bab pa'i gzugs phun sum tshogs pa/sngan chad ma btsas pa/bu dang bu mo myed pa/gzugs mdzes pa/bu don la bris pa blta na sdug pa/lha'i bu mo ltar rgyan thams cad kyis brgyand pa/bud myed
15b8(1): kyi skyon rnams dang bral ba/bden par smra ba/myi brlang ba/myi gcod pa/kha na ma tho ba myed pa/gyo mo'i sgra lta bu myed pa/ca co myed pa/'jam zhing snyan la yid du 'ong bar smra ba/myi des pa dang khong khro ba dang/
15b9(1): nga rgyal dang/dregs pa dang/rgyags pa dang/khong khro ba myed pa dang/phra dog myed pa dus su smra ba /gtong ba phun sum tshogs pa/tshul khrims dang ldan ba/khyos chog par 'dzind pa/khyim thab kyi rtul (zhab)can /skyes pa gzhan
15b10(1): la bsam pa'i yid la bya ba dang blan pa/mgo dang/rna ba dang/sna gnas su phebs pa/phung po mchog gi kha dog lta bu'i skra dang ldan ba/spral ba legs ba/smyin skyugs legs pa/khro gnyer myed pa/bzhin
16a1(1): @//'dzum ba dang/gsong bor smra ba/'jam zhing snyan ba'i tshig dang ldan ba/mthun bar 'dzind pa dang/gya gyu myed pa dang/sgyu myed pa/g.yo myed pa/khrel yod cing ngo tsha shes pa/myi rgod pa/myi g.yeng ba/kha ngan
16a2(1): myi smra ba/'chal ba'i tshig myed pa/'dod chags dang zhe sdang dang/gti mug gi shas chung ba/dad nges shing bzod ba dang ldan ba/rka lag dang myig dang/blo shind tu bsrungs pa/rka lag gzhon zhing 'jam pa/ka tsa lin
16a3(1): di ltar reg na bde ba/bad ma gzho nu'i 'dab ma ltar myig shind tu rnam par dag pa/sna'i dbyibs legs shing mdog sdug ba/yan lag shind tu brtan ba/'jim ba'i dbyangs ltar shin tu 'dud pa /yan lag dang
16a4(1): nying lag shind tu dbyes pa/yan lag la smad du myed pa/shing tog byil pa lta bu'i mchu dang ldan ba/blta na mdzes pa/'gul rim bzhin du 'dug pa/rgyan dam pa dang ldan ba/me tog u ma na dang/
16a5(1): par shi ka dang/so shind tu rnam par dag pa/phrag ba legs par byind kyis gzhol ba/rim gyis legs par grub pa'i dbung dang ldan ba/lto gzhung ltar 'dug pa/gzhog nyams pa myed pa
16a6(1): lte ba khung zab ba dang/sta zur 'jam zhing yangs la blum zhing brtan bar grub pa/rdo rje ltar mkhregs shing mtshungs pa myed pa'i lus dang ldan ba/glang bo'i sna ltar mnyam zhing legs pI
16a7(1): brla dang ldan ba/ri dgas e ne ya'i byin pa lta bu dang ldan ba/lag pa dang rkang pa'i mthil rgya skyegs kyi khu ba ltar 'dug pa/'gro ba'i myig tu 'dug pa/myig gi dbang po nyams pa myed pa/yid du
16a8(1): 'ong zhing lta na sdug pa/bud myed rin po che'i gzugs las kyang khyad par du 'phags ba/mtshungs pa myed pa/sgyu mas spruld pa lta bu'i gzugs su 'dug pa/sgyu 'phrul zhes mying gi brdar btags pa/
16a9(1): sgyu rtsal la mkhas pa dag dga' 'atsal gyi lha'i bu mo lta bu zhig rgyal po zas gtsang ma'i btsun mo 'khor gyi nang na 'dug pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i yum du tshul dang mthun gyis/gang byang chub sems dpa'i rigs
16a10(1): dag pa bstand pa ni/shag kya'i rigs nyid la snang gi gzhan la ma yin no//de la 'di skad ces bya ste/dag pa'i sems can chos mthong khang bzangs na/chos bzang seng ge'i khri la rab tu 'dug/byang chub ses
16b1(1): dpa' grags chen dang ldan ba dang/skal ba mnyam pa'i lha rnams drang srong 'khord/de nas 'dug cing de ni 'di snyam sems//rigs gang rnam dag rab tu shes dang ldan//byang chub sems dpa' skyed dang tshul 'thun gang//
16b2(1): sa ma gang zhig dag pa'i chos dang ldan//'dzam bu'i gling 'di kun tu rnam ltas na/rgyal rigs rgyud tshe ba gang yin ba/kun kyang skyon dang bcas par rjes bsams nas//shag kya'i rigs ni skyon dang bral bar mthong/zas gtsang 
16b3(1): rgyal bo rigs nang rigs dang ldan//myi dbang rgyud ni rnam dag cho rigs can//'byord cing rgyas la 'khrug pa myed pa ste/skye bo dam pa chos ldan rim gro can/ser skyar brjod pa'i grong khyer sems can gzhan/kun kyang 
16b4(1): bsam pa rab dag chos dang ldan//skyed mos kun dga' gzhal myed khang gis brgyand//skye ba'i sa gzhi ser skyer brjod pa mdzes/kun kyang mtsho chen stobs dang ldan ba ste/glang po gnyis sam gsum gyi 
16b5(1): stobs kyang bdog//'phong rtsal bslabs pa'i pha rold mtharphyind kyang//srog gi don tu gzhan la gnod myi byed//zas gatsang rgyal po'i bung myed dgyen ba de/bud myed dam pa stong gi mchog 
16b6(1): tu phyind//yid du 'ong ba sgyu ma sku lad pa'i gzugs/de'i mying mying yang lha mo sgyu 'phrul te/mdzes pa'i gzugs 'chang lha'i bu mobzhin/dgyes pa'i lus ldan yan lag dri myed sdug/lha dang myi rnaMs
16b7(1): gang yang rung ba dag/sgyu 'phrul mthong na gang ngoms de myed kyang/'dod chags zhen myed sdang ba'i zhe sdang myed//de ni 'jam snyam Iba'i ngag dang ldan//myi brlang myi thub shin tu des pa ste/de ni bzhin 'dzum khro gnyer rab
16b8(1): tu spangs/ngo tsha khrel yod chos spyod ldan ba ste/nga rgyal myed cing ma khengs rgod pa myed//phra dog myed cing sgyu dang g.yo' myed de//gtong bar dga' zhing rtag tu byams sems ldan/las yod ldan zhing log pa'i sbyor spangs//
16b9(1): bden la gnas shing lus dang yid rab sngangs//sa la gang yod bud myed skyon mang tshogs//de dag thams cad de la yong ma yin//myi'i 'jig rten dri za'i 'jig rten nas//lha'i 'jig rten dag na bud myed gzhan//
16b10(1): lha mo sgyu 'phrul mtshungs myed ga la lhag//drang srong chen po mar ni de 'os te//tshe rabs lnga brgya kho na tshang Ipar yang//de ni byang chub sems dpa' mar gyurd to//de dang der ni yab kyang zas gtsang ste//de phyir yon tan 
17a1(1): //ldan pa ma'i 'os//dka' thub can ltar de ni rtul zhugs can//brtul zhugs rjes spyod rtag par chos kyang spyod//rgyal pos gnang ste mchog sbyin thos pas na//gang gi phyogs na de 'dug 'greng ba 'am//nyal 
17a2(1): zhing 'gro bar gyurd pa'i gnas dag kyang//de'i las dag gzhol ba'i 'od zer gyis//shind tu snang bar 'os kyis gsal bar 'gyur//lha dang lha myin myi rnams gang yang rung//'dod chags sems kyis blta bar nus pa myed//
17a3(1): spyod lam rnam gsum 'phags pa'i yon tan ldan//de la kun kyang ma dad bu mor lta//sgyu 'phrul lha mo'i dge' ba'i las kyi rgyus//rgyal po'i srid ni rab yangs rnam par 'phel//khams kyi rgyal por 'ang 'jug par myi byed cing/rgyal 
17a4(1): po'i grags dang brjod pa rnam par 'phel//de ltar sgyu 'phrul snod du mthun gyurd cing//de ltar sems can dam pa mchog mdzes pas//de gnyis yon tan mchog dang ldan pa nas//'di ni bur 'gyur de ni ma'i ngos//lha mo 
17a5(1): yon tan mchog dang ldan ba ste//glang po'i khri'i stobs mdog ma ga togs par//'dzam bu'i rgyal mtshan 'di ni mchog de//'dzind par nus pa'i bud myed gang yang myed//de skad che ba'i lha'i rnams 
17a6(1): dang//byang chub sems dpa' shes rab rgya chen dag//shag kyi'i rigs rnams dga' bar mdzad pa'i//yum du legs ldan sgyu 'phrul 'gyur zhes zer////
17a7(1): //rigs yongs su dag pa'i le'u ste/gsum po'o////dge slong de ltar byang chub sems dpa' skye ba'i rigs rnam par ltas na/dga' ldan gyi gnas mchog 
17a8(1): gi gzhal myed khang chen po'i chu dang rgyang dpag tshad drug bcu rtsa bzhi yod pa gang tu/byang chub sems dpa' 'dug ste/dga' ldan gyi lha rnams la chos stond pa rgyal mtshan mthon po zhes bya ba yod de/
17a9(1): byang chub sems dpa' de gzhal myed khang chen po des bsod nams phyind pa dang/dga' ldan gyi ris kyi lha'i bu thams cad la smras pa/khyed rnams 'dir 'du bar gyis shig//shi 'pho'i rnam par sbyor ba zhes bya ba 
17a10(1): chos rjes su bstand pa tha ma'i chos nyand pa byang chub sems dpa' las nyon cig/de skad kyi tshig thos nas dga' ldan ris kyi lha'i bu thams can lha'i bu mo'i tshogs bcas te/gzhal myed khang chen po 'dir 'dus so//
17b1(1): byang chub sems dpa' 'khord kyi khyams gling bzhi ba chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi tshad tsam du yangs par byind kyis brlabs par gyurd nas/de de tsam du bzang zhing de tsam du blta na sdug pe da tsam du rgyand kyis rgyas shing de tsam du yid du
17b2(1): 'ong bar gyurd pa des/'dod pa na spyod pa dang gzugs na spyod pa'i lha'i bu thams cad rang rang gi khyim gyi bkod pa la dur khrod lta bu'i 'du shes skyes par gyurd to//de la byang chub sems dpa' rang gi bsod nams kyi rnam par smyind
17b3(1): pa'i rgyu mthun bas/yongs su rgyand pa'i gser gyi khri nor bu dang rin po che du mas spras pa/lha'i sar bcos ma'i stan du ma bting ba/lha'i spos zhim po du mas bskord pa/bdug spos dam pa mchog tu bya ba du mas gdugs
17b4(1): pa/lha'i me tog kha dog du ma bgra ba bkram pa nor bu dang/rin po che'i dril bu brgya stong du ma 'bar ba'i gzi brjid can/nor bu dang rin po che du mi'i dra bas g.yogs pa/rin po che'i dril bu g.yer ka'i dra ba du ma'i sgra
17b5(1): byung bas mngon bar dga' ba dang ldan pa/rin po che'i dril bub rgya stong du ma mngon bar dgrol ba'i sgra dang ldan ba/rin po che brgya stong du mas yongs su gsal bar gyurd ba/rin po che'i gdugs
17b6(1): brgya stong du mas kun nas bkang ba/dar brgya stong du mas rab tu sbyangs pa/dar gyi phreng ba'i ldang lding brgya stong du mas shind tu rgyand pa/lha'i bu mo brgya stong du mas glu blangs gar byas rol mo byas
17b7(1): yon tan brgya stong du ma kun tu brjod pa/'jig rten skyong ba brgya stong du mas rjes su bsrungs pa/brgya byin brgya stong du mas phyag byas pa/tshangs pa brgya stong du mas rab tu 'dud pa/byang chub sems dpa' bye ba khrag
17b8(1): khrig brgya stong du mas yongs subazung bas/phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du ma mtha' yas bas/dgongs pa/bskald pa bye ba khrag khrig brgyad stong dpag tu myed pa pha rold tu phyind pa/tshogs
17b9(1): su bsdus pa'i bsod nams kyi rnam par smyind pa cha mthun ba las byung ba de lta bu la 'dug go//dge slong dag de ltar byang chub sems dpa' seng ge'i khri chen po yon tan de lta bu dang ldan pa la 'dug nas/lha'i 'khor chen po de dag
17b10(1): la smras pa/grogs po dag byang chub sems dpa'i lus bsod nams brgya'i mtshan kyis legs par brgyand pa la ltos/shar dang lho dang nub dang byang dang 'og dang steng dang phyogs bcu kun na/byang chub sems dpa' dga'
18a1(1): //ldan gyi gnas dam pa na 'khod cing/thams cad kyang srid pa tha ma la mngon tu phyogs pa /lha'i tshogs kyis yongs su bsngord pa/shi 'pho'i rnam pas lha rnams dga' bar byed pa'i chos yang ba'i sgo yang dag par
18a2(1): stond pa tshad myed grangs myed pa las 'das pa dag la ltos/de nas lha'i bu 'khor de dag thams cad kyis byang chub sems dpa'i byind kyi rlabs kyis /byang chub sems dpa' de dag mthong bar gyurd te/mthong bar gyurd nas kyang
18a3(1): byang chub sems dpa' ga la ba der logasu thal mo sbyard pa btud de/yan lag lngas phyag byas nas/'di skad du 'di ltar bdag gis bltas pa btsal gyis/byang chub sems dpa' 'di snyed cig mthong ba ni byang chub seMs
18a4(1): dpa'i byin rlabs bsam gyis myi khyab ste legs so zhes ched du brjod pa ched du brjod do//de byang chub sems dpa' yang lha'i 'khor chen po de dag la bos nas 'di skad ces smras so//grogs po de bas na byang
18a5(1): chub sems dpa' de dag lha'i bu de dag la gang stond pa/shi 'pho'i rnam par lha rnams dga' bar byed pa'i chos snang ba'i sgo nyon cig//grogs po dag chos snang ba'i brgya rtsa brgyad po 'di dag ni/byang chub
18a6(1): sems dpa' shi 'pho'i dus la bab pa'i tshe/lha'i 'khor la gdon myi za bar yang dag par bstand par bya ste brgya rtsa brgyad gang zhe na 'di lta ste/grogs po dad pa ni chos snang ba'i sgo ste bsam bar
18a7(1): myi byed do/dang ba ni chos snang ba'i sgo ste/sems rnyog pa can dang par 'gyur ro//mchog tu dga' ba ni chos snang ba'i sgo nye lus shind tu sbyangs par 'gyur ro//dga' ba ni chos snang ba'i so ste sems rnam par dag par
18a8(1): 'gyur ro//lus kyi sdom ba ni chos snang ba'i sgo ste/rnam gsum yongs su dag par 'gyur ro//ngag gi sdom ba ni chos snang pa'i sgo ste/ngag gi nyes pa bzhi yongs su spong bar 'gyur ro//yid kyi sdom ba ni chos
18a9(1): snang ba'i sgo ste/brnab sems dang/gnod sems dang/log par lta ba spong bar 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas rjes su dran ni chos snang ba'i sgo ste/sangs rgyas mthong ba rnam par dag par 'gyur ro//chos rjes su dran ba
18a10(1): ni chos snang ba'i sgo ste/chos bstand pa rnam par dag par 'gyur ro//dge 'dun rjes su dran ba ni chos snang ba'i sgo skye/skyon myed pa rab tu 'jug par 'gyur ro//gtong ba rjes su dran pa ni chos snang ba'i sgo ste rjes thams cad
18b1(1): rab tu spong bar 'gyur ro//tshul khrims rjes su dran ba'i ni chos snang ba'i sgo ste/smon lam yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro//lha rjes su dran ba ni chos snang ba'i sgo ste/sems rgya chen por 'gyur ro /byams ba ni chos snang ba'i sgo
18b2(1): ste/rdzas las byung ba'i bsod nams bya ba'i dngos po thams cad kyis gnond par 'gyur ro//snying rje ni chos snang ba'i sgo ste /rnam par gtse ba myed pa lhur lend par 'gyur ro//dga' ba ni chos snang ba'i sgo ste /myi dga'
18b3(1): ba thams cad seld par 'gyur ro//btang snyoms ni chos snang ba'i sgo ste/'dod pa la smod par 'gyur ro/myi rtag par so sor rtog pa ni chos snang ba'i sgo ste//'dod pa ni gzugs yod pa dang /gzugs myed pa'i 'dod chags
18b4(1): las yang dag par 'gyur ro//sdug sngal so sor rtog ba ni chos snang ba'i sgo ste/rjes su chags pa myed par 'gyur ro//ngo tsha shes pa ni chos snang ba'i sgo /nang nye bar zhi bar 'gyur ro//khrel yod pa ni
18b5(1): par 'gyur ro//zhi bar so sor rtog pa'i chos snang ba'i sgo ste/rjes su chags pa myed mar 'gyur ro//ngo tsha shes ba ni chos snang ba'i sgo ste/nang nye bar zhi bar 'gyur ro//khrel yod ba ni

23a2(1): dam ba de dag na mar dang 'bru mar dang/sbrang rtsi dang/gu ram gyi dbu' ba dang/sha ka ra las stsogs pa bza' ba rnam pa gang yod pa de dag kyang yongs su spyad pas zad par myi 'gyur zhing/yongs su gang bar snang ste/'di ni snga ltas 
23a3(1): 'byung ba lnga ba'o//rgyal po zas rtsang ma'i khyim mchog dam pa dang/btsun mo'i 'khor chen po dag na rnga bo che dang rdza rnga dang kha rnga dang /rgyud cig pa dang/pi wang dang/gling bu dang/rgyud gsum pa dang/khar ba'i sil

23a5(1): dam pa na gser dang dngul dang nor bu dang mu tig dang be du r+ya dang dung dang/man shel dang/pi ru dang/stsogs pa rin po che sna tshogs kyi snod gang dag yod pa de dagthams cad kyang kha bye zhing dri ma myed la/rnam
23a6(1): bar dag cing rab tu gang bar snang ste/'di na snga ltas 'byung ba bdun pa'o//nyi zla'i 'od zild tu rlags par byed cing/lus dang sems dpa' ba skyed pa'i 'od dag cing dri ma myed pas snang bar
23a7(1): gyurd te/'di ni snga ltas 'byur ba brgyad pa'o//lha mo sgyu 'phrul yang khrus byas lus byug pas byugs/rgyan sna tshogs kyis lag pa shind tu brgyand te/gos shind tu 'jam pa'i mchog bgos
23a8(1): shing/btsun spu dang bcas la/dga' ba dang/mchog tu dga' ba dang/rangs pa thob nas bud myed stong phrug bcus bskord cing mdun tu bdard te/lhan cig tu rgyal po zas rtsang ma'i rol so bya ba'i khang bzangs na bde bar
23a9(1): gnas pa'i drung du phyind te/g.yas rol nas khri bzang po rin po che'i dra bas brgyand pa la 'dug nas/'dzum pa'i bzhin ni khro gnyer myed cing rab tu dang ba'i mdog gis//rgyal po zas rtsang ma la tshigs su bcad pa 'di
23a10(1): dag smras so//rgyal po sa bdag legs par bdag la gsan tu gsol//rgyal po khyed la gsol gyis bdag la de gnong shig//bdag gi bsam pa yid la ci mchis ci dga' ba//de dag bdag la gsand cing thugs
23b1(1): dgyes rangs par mdzod//lha bdag nyer gnas tshul khrims rtul zhugs lend par gsol//bdag ni 'gro la byams pas yan lag brgyad lend te//srog chags gnod pa spangs shing bsam ba rtag dag pas//ji ltar bdag la byaMs
23b2(1): de ltar gzhan la bgyi//rku sems rnam par spangs te 'dod dang dregs pa spang//rgyal po 'dod pa rnams la log par g.yem myi bgyid//bden la gnas shing pha ma ma mchis zhe gcod spang//myi dge kyal pa'i tshig kyang yongs
23b3(1): su myi 'byod do//gnod sems zhe sdang thab pa gti mug rgyags pa spang//brnab sems kun spang rang gi nor gyis 'tshal bar gyis//yang dag sbyor bas kha gsag tshul chos phra dog spang//dge bcu'i las lam 'di dag
23b4(1): bdag gis spyad par bgyi//bdag ni tshul khrims brtul zhugs mdona bar rab ldan gyis//myi'i dbang po bdag la zhend pas sred ma mdzad//rgyal po khyod la yun ring myi dger gyurd tu mchi//bdag gi tshul khris
23b5(1): brtul zhugs nyer gnas dgyes par mdzod//rgyal po bdag gis sa 'tshald pa 'di lags der myur gsol//khang bzangs bsi khang ldan dang skya ldan ba'i nang//me tog bkram ba'i mchis lam 'jam
23b6(1): zhing dri gda' bar//grogs mos rtag tu bskord cing bde dgar gnas snyam bgyid//skyes bu nyung rum rnams dang de bzhin khye'u dag dang bud myed phal pa rnams kyang mdun na mchis myi 'tshal//snyand
23b7(1): cing yid mchi sgra brnyan nyand pa ma gtogs par//gzugs sgra gsung yid myi mchi ba bdag myi 'tshal//bcings dang btson rar bstsald ba thams cad dgyes par gsol//nor gyis 'breld pa'i myi rnams nor dang ldan bar
23b8(1): mchod//zas skom rad ba shing rta phyogs dang gzhon ba rta//'gro la bde ba'i slad du rgur bdun stsald par mdzod//pho brang 'di na skyes pa khye'u bud myed dag/ci snyed mchis pa thab rtsod khro ba'i tshig bral zhing//phan tshun
23b9(1): byams pa'i yid ldan phan dang des sems kyi//dga' mtshal gnas pa'i lha ltar mthun zhing bder spyod mdzod//rgyal po'i chad pa che phra gnyer gyis bda' ba dang//gnod bar bgyid rdeg gachog sdig tu myi mdzad//kun la
23b10(1): 'ang dang ba'i sems kyis byams dang phan 'dogs te//lha'i skye bo rnams la sras cig bzhin du gzigs//rgyal pos de skad tshig thos rab tu dgar gyurd nas//smras pa khyod ni ji 'dod de kun de bzhin bya//khyod kyi
24a1(1): #/ O /bsam ba yid kyis gang rnams rjes bsams shing //khyod kyis mchog bslangs gang yin de ni da Isa sbyin no//rgyal po'i dam pas rang gi 'khor la bsko ba ni//khang bzangs dam pa'i steng du phun sum tshogs par bshoms//

38a10(1): lha mo sgyu 'phrul gcig bu 'dug gyis //bud myed skyes ba gzhan dag su yang shing rta der ma khong na chung sna tshogs go chan rnams kyis shing rta de drongs te//gang tu 'ang yid du myi 'ong myi mthun sgra rnams myi gag gyis//

38b3(1): me tog lo ma ltan bzhi ldan par byas//ma bya khrung khrung dang ba yid du 'ong ba'i sgra yang 'byind//'dugs dang brgyal mtshan ba dan tshe 'phra kun nas bsgreng //lha'i gos dang dril bu g.yer ka'i tra ba mchog kyis bkab//lha'i bu mo nam ka

38b5(1): ba mo 'byind cing me tog sna tshogs 'thord//de ring lum bi ni 'dir 'gro ba'i dam pa bltam gyurd te//'jig rten bskyong ba bzhi ni shing rta'i mtsho ga de 'dren bar byed//sum bcu rtsa gsum bdag po dbang po 'ang

38b7(1): de ni 'di snyam 'di ni lha'i lha zhig yin snyam byed gang la skyong ba bzhi dang tshangs pa dbang por bcas pa'i lha//mo chad pa rgya chen byed pa 'di ni des bar sangs rgyas 'gyur//srid pa gsum na lha klu brgya byind tshangs pa 'jig rten skyong //
38b8(1): gang yang mo chad pa 'di 'dra bzo'i sems can de myed de//gang yang mgo 'gos yang na de dag srog dang bral bar gyurd//'di ni lha'i dam pa yid bas mchod pa thams cad bzod//dge slong drag de nas lha mo sgyu 'phrul rta'i shing rta brgyan

39a1(1): @#///bar ma bzhi khris ni yongs su bsrungs //rgyal po zas gtsang ma pa'i nas /klu dang rol mo'i dbyangs sgra byed/khar ba'i sil khror dang/phyeg rdob /gsil snyan dang /glu dbyangs lend pa drug khris ni kun

39a10(1): brten ba/lha'i klu byangs blangs pa/gnas gtsang ma'i lha bzang zhing dri ma myed la rnam bar dag pa rab tu bzhi ba'i sems dang ldan ba rnams kyis thor tshugs dang cod pan mngon bar btud pa'i sbyi pos phyag

39b4(1): tshang nas ma'i glo g.yas pa nas dran zhing shes pa bzhin du mngal gyi dri mas ma gos bar byung ste/gzhan ltar ma yin zhing gzhan dag la mngal gyi dri ma zhes bya ba brjod pa lta bu myed bar gyurd to//dge slong dag de'i tshe lha'i dbang 
39b5(1): po brgya byind dang/myi mdzed gi bdag po tshangs pa gnyis bdun na 'khod do/de gnyis kyi sa la rim gro dam pa pa chen pos lha'i gos ka shi ka'i sleb rmong lus dang yan lag thams cad kyis dran zhing

51a3(1): ba lags so//rgyal po chen po gzhonu don thams cad grub pa'i dpe' byad bzang po yongs su rdzogs pa ste/phyag ring ba dang/sku'i na bza' gtsang ma phun sum tshogs pa dang/sku 'jam dang/snyengs myi mnga'
51a4(1): ba'i sku dang/zhum pa myi mnga' ba'i sku dang/rim pa bzhin du gnas pa'i sku dang/shind tu spang bar mdzes pa'i sku dang/rnam pa legs par grub pa'i sku dang/pus mo'i lha nga yangs shing rgya che la shind tu
51a5(1): gang ba dang ldan pa lags so//rgyal po chen bo gzho nu don thams cad grub pa ni sku zlum pa dang ldan pa ste/sku shind tu 'jam pa dang/sku la yo ba mchis pa dang/sku rim gyis legs par grub
51a6(1): pa dang/lte ba zab pa dang/lte ba ma yo ba dang/lte ba rims ky-is gzhol ba dang/drang srong ltar gtsang sbras spyod pa dang/kunas nas mdzes pa dang/snang ba dam pa shind tu rnam par dag cing rab rib
51a7(1): ma mchis pa'i 'od kun nas 'byung ba dang ldan pa lags so //rgyal po chen po gzho nu don thams cad 'grub pa ni glang po ltar dal ba'i 'gro sa dang/seng ge'i stabs lta bu'i 'gros dang/khyu mchog gi stabs lta bu'i 'gros dang /rang
51a8(1): pa'i stabs su ba'i 'gros dang/g.yas phyogs su mdzes par 'khor ba'i 'gros dang/phyal zlum pa dang/phyal byang ba dang/phyal rnyad rnyod myed pa dang/phyal gzhung ltar 'dug pa dang/mdog gi nyes pa bsngo ba 'am/gnag pa'i
51a9(1): skyon dang bral ba'i sku dang ldan pa lags so//rgyal po chen po gzhonu thams cad grub pa ni che ba zlum dang/che ba rno ba dang/che ba byind kyis phra ba dang/shangs legs par mtho ba dang/spyan gsal ba dang/spyan dri ma myed pa dang
51a10(1): 'jum pa'i spyan dang ldan pa dang/spyan ring ba dang/spyan yangs pa dang/ud pa la sngon po lta bu'i spyan dang ldan pa lags so//rgyal po chen po gzho nu non thams cad grub pa ni smyin ma mnyam pa dang/smyin ma sdug pa dang/smyin
51b1(1): ma nag pa dang/sbyin ma rgyun chags pa dang/sbyin ma rims kyis gzhol ba dang/mkhur ba rgyas pa dang/'khur ba nyams pa myed pa dang/mkhur ba skyon dang bral dang/sdang ba dang khros pa'i mdog myed pa dang/shind 
51b2(1): rig pa'i dbang po dang ldan pa lags so//rgyal po chen po gzho nu don thams cad grub pa'i mdzod spu yengs (du )(dag)pa dang ldan ba dang/gdong dang 'phral ba'i rkyend tu 'phags pa dang/dbu yongs su rgyas pa dang/dbu' skra gnag pa
51b3(1): dang/dbu skra mnyam ba dang/dbu skra dri zhim dang/dbul skra myi gzhol ba dang/dbu skra myi 'dzing ba dang/skra byind kyis phra ba dang/dbu skra lcang lor 'khyild pa dang ldan ba lags so//rgyal po chen pa 
51b4(1): gzho nu don thams cad grab pa ni dbu skra dpal gyi be'u dang kra shis dang/g.yung rung 'khyild pa dang/sdong ris kyi dbyibs lta bu'i dbu skra dang ldan ba'o//rgyal po chen po 'di dag ni gzho nu don thams cad 'grub pa'i
51b5(1): dpe' byad bzang po brgyad cu ste/de dag dang ldan bas gzho nu don thams cad sgrub pa ni khab na bzhugs (pa)myi 'gyur gyi/gdon (               )myi 'tshal bar rab tu byung ba'i slad du khab nas 'byung bar 'gyur ro/
51b6(1): de nas rgyal po zas cang mas drang srong chen po nag po las gzho nu'i lung stand pa de thos nas tshim zhing mgu la yid rangs pa de rab tu dga' zhing/dga' ba dang yid bde ba skyes nas/stan las langs te/byang chub ses
51b7(1): dba'i rkang pa gnyis la phyag 'tshal nas/tshigs su bcad pa 'di smras so//khyod la lha kun phyag 'tshal zhing/drang srong rnams kyis mchod 'gyurd te/'jig rten kun kyis mchod pas bdag kyang khyod la phyag
51b8(1): 'tshal lo//dge slong dag de ltar rgyal po zas gcang mas drang srong chen ponag po dang/snag gi tsha bo myis byin dang/bcas te kha zas tshul dang 'dra bas tshim bar byas nas gos bskyond te/bskor ba byas so//de nas drang
51b9(1): srong chen po nag pos gnas de nyid kyang rdzu 'phrul gyis nam mkha' la rang gi gnas ga la ba der song ngo//de nas drang srong chen po nag pas bram ze'i khye'u myis (kyin)la 'di skad ces smras so//myis sbyin khyod kyis nam sangs rgyas 'jig rten
52a1(1): //tu//tu 'byung ngo zhes thos pa de'i tshe khyod song ste/de'i stand pa la rab tu phyung shig dang/de ni khyod kyis yun ring po'i don dang phan pa dang bde bar 'gyur ro//de la 'di skad ces bya ste/lha'i tshogs nam mkha'i ngos la gnas te/
52a2(1): sangs rgyas kyi sgra sgrogs pa//lha'i drang srong nag po ri sul gnas pas 'thon nas dga' mchog thob//'di na srog chags rnams kyi dga' skyed sangs rgyas zhes bya'i tshig 'di ci//bdag gi lus kyang rab tu sim gyurd cing 
52a3(1): sems can bde ba mchog tu zhi bar gyurd//de lha 'am 'on tam lha myin yang na nam mkha' lding ngam myi'am ci//sangs rgyas zhes bya dga' ba dang/mos byed tshig 'di sngan chad ma thos ci//lha'i myig gis phyogs bcur
52a4(1): de dang sa dang rgya mtsho rnams la bltas//phyis kyang sa ri mtsho ya rmad du byung ba rnam pa sna tshogs mthong//'od gsal mdzes pa lus la sim byed 'di ni rab tu bzang snang zhing//ji ltar ri'i rtse mo rnams su byu ru'i myu gu mthun
52a5(1): gyurd la//shing rjond rnams kyang me tog bzang rgyas sna tshogs 'bras bus rgyand gyurd las//srid pa gsum du dkon mchog dam pa myurd tu 'byung bar shin tu sal//sa 'di thams cad mnyam zhing
52a6(1): dri myed lag thil mazhin du snang ba dang/de nas lha rnams dga' zhing nam mkha'i dkyil na gos kyi g.yab mo 'byind gyurd la//klu rgyal rgya mtsho'i gnas kyi steng na rin cen rmad 'byung 'phyo ba las/
52a7(1): 'dzam bu'i gling du rgyal ba'i dkon mchog chos kyi 'byung gnas nges par 'byung//ngan song zhi zhing sdug bsngal bral te sems can rnams ni bde ldan la//lha'i tshogs nam mkha'i ngos la dga' dang ldan par
52a8(1): rgyu ba dang//ji ltar lha'i klu dbyangs sgra snyan yid du 'ong pa grag pa las//srid pa gsum 'dir dkon mchog 'byung 'gyur de'i bltas su gdon myi za//'dzam bu'i gling 'dir drang srong nag pos lha'i myig gis rnam
52a9(1): bltas na/ser skya'i grong na rgyal po zas gtsang grong khyer dam pa yod pa der//mthu chen mthu ldan mtshan dang bsod nams gzi brjid rgyas pa btsas pa mthong//mthong nas dga' mgu rangs shing bde ba'i yid bskyed
52a10(1): mthu' yang skyes par gyurd//de nas ngo mtshar rnyed de rang gi slob ma lhan cig rings su dong//phyi nas ser skya'i brjod pa'i rgyal po'i grong khyer mchog gi sgor 'jug ste//zham ring srog chags bye ba khrag khrig mang po
52b1(1): drang srong de mthong nas//sko ba de la smras pa chab sgor drang srong phyin ces myurd tu gsol//de mnyan myurd tu myur tu pho brang nas phyind rgyal po la de bsnyan ba//lhan cig drang srong rgan 'khogs dka' thub chen po can zhig chab 
52b2(1): sgor mchis//drang srong mchog de rgyal po'i pho brang nang du mchi bar dga' zhes mchi//rgyal po chen po'i nang du btang dam myi btang bdag la bka' stsald par gsol//de nas de'i stan bting rgyal po khyod song nang du mthong zhes bsgo'
52b3(1): dran srong nag po sgo ba'i tshig thos dga' mgu bde ba dang ldan bar gyurd//bskom ba gsil ba'i chu 'dod bkres pas gdungs pas zas la mos pa ltar//drang srong dam pa de yang sems can mchog de mthong du mngon bar dga' na/
52b4(1): rgyal po rgyal 'gyur sku tshe ring bsrung gsol zhes dga' bas de skad smras//bsnyand bar brjod nas yid dul dbang po zhi zhing des pa stan la 'dug/rgyal po mngon par smras byas drang srong thub pa de la tshig brjod pa//
52b5(1): rgyal po'i pho brang 'dir byond rgyu ci thub pas bdag la myurd tu gsungs//khyod kyi sras bltam gzugs mchog pha rold phyind cing gzi brjid che dang ldan//mthu cen mtshan rab sum bcu rtsa gnyis kyis
52b6(1): kun nas brgyand mnga'/rgyal po khyod sras thams cad don grub de ni mthong bar bdag dga' ste//de slad 'dir mchis rgyal po bdag la' tshal ba gzhan ni yong ma mchis//legs so legs par byond
52b7(1): to mnyeld tam ma mnyel khyod mthong bdag dga'o//mchog sbyin gzho nu de nyal da ltar blta ba'i skabs ni mchis gyis//res shig yud tsam bzhes dang 'dir ni zla ba nya ba dri myed pa//skar ma'i tshogs kyis brgyand pa zla ba'i dri
52b8(1): myed gzigs par 'gyur//gang tshe kha lo sgyur mchog zla ba rgyas pa'i 'od mnga' de sad pha//de tshe rgyal pos 'od 'bar gzugs 'chang gnyi las 'phags 'od phang blangs ste//'di ltar drang srong lha myin mchod pa gser mchog
52b9(1): gzugs ldan da gzigs shig//drang srong nag pos de'i rkang pa mdzes pa 'khor lo'i mtshan can mthong//de nas langs te thal mo sbyard nas rkang pa gnyis la phyag 'tshal te//bdag nyid chen po stan chos mkhas pas
52b10(1): pang du blangs nas bsams shing bltas//des ni mthu' chen mthu' ldan mtshan kyi mchog gis brgyand pa mthong gyurd nas/mgo bcogs rig byed bstan chos mkhas pa des ni de la rnam gnyis mthong//stobs ldan
53a1(1): @/ O /'khor los sgyur bo 'am sangs rgyas 'jig rten rtso bor 'gyur//shind tu myi dga' lus dang sems kyis mchi ma blags te shugs ring phyung//rgyal po mchog kyang sngags par gyurd nas bram ze ci slad bshus
53a2(1): ka shes smra/drang srong nag pos bdag gi don grub bgegs ma gzigs sam/drang srong ci slad bshums mdzad de dag sdig pa gang gzigs bdag la mang par gsungs//khyod sras don kun grub la bar chad sdig tu 'gyur ba ma

53b9(1): thob par 'gyur ro/rgyal po zas gtsang ma 'phel ba yang rjes su sgrag par 'gyur ro//byang chub sems dpa' yang de kho na'i lung bstand te/bdag cag phyir 'ong bar bya'o/de skad ces bsgo nas lha'i bu dbang

54a7(1): gtsug ni legs ldan spyod lam ldan//gtsug phud mdzes pa 'phyang ba rnam par dag pa'i lha//stong phrag bcu gnyis tshang ba gserskya'i gnas mchog tu//myurd tu lhags nas rgyal po'i sgo drung na

54b3(1): rgyal pos de skad chig thos rab tu dgar 'gyur nas//song ste kun kyang pho brang nang du gshegs shes kyis//de dag yon tan spyod lam khyod kyis smras ltar na//rdzu 'phrul de 'dra ba ni myi la gang yang myed sgo bas

55a2(1): mul sel nyon mongs bral ba'i yon tan brtan nas su//yon tan ji ltar de mthong ba la ston//skye dang na dang 'chi dang nyon mongs dag stsald pa'i//sems can rin cen 'di ni ring por myi thogs 'byung//yongs 
55a3(1): rtog 'dod chags yul gyi gtsug mas skyed pa'i//rnam gsum mes ni srid gsum kun 'bar yongs gdungs par//dpa' khyod chos kyi sprin gyis stong gsum bkang nas ni//'chi myed char gyi nyon mongs gdung ba
55a4(1): ba zhi bar 'gyur//byams pa'i gsung mnga' thugs rje ldan ba 'jam pa'i dbyangs//tshangs pa'i sgra dbyangs yid 'ong gsung du sgrogs mdzad pa//stong gsum kun tu 'gro zhing 'gro la grol 'gyur ba//bcom

73b3(1): yun ring po nas /'du shes dang/chos shes pa dang/ran bar shes par gyurd pa dang/mngon bar shes pa las ma nyams pa dang/mngon bar shesapa lnga dang ldan pa dang/rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa las rnam par brtse ba
73b4(1): dang/sems can gyi bas pa ba dang/dbang po la mkhas dang/dus dang dus ma yin pa shes pa dang/dus la lta zhing/rgya mtsho chen po ltar dus/ran ba las myi yol bar gyurd pa'o//
73b5(1): de mngon bar shes pa'i ye shes kyi stobs dang ldan pas /'di ni rab tu gzung ba'i dus so//'di ni tshar bcad pa'i dus so//'di ni bsdu ba'i dus so//'di phan gdags pa'i dus so
73b6(1): 'di ni btang snyoms su bzhag pa'i dus so//'di ni smra ba'i dus so/'di ni myi smra ba'i dus so//'di ni khyim nas byung ba'i dus so//'di ni rab tu byung ba'i dus so//'di na kha ton
73b7(1): du bya ba'i dus so//'di nitshul bzhin du yid la bya ba'i dus so//'dirab tu dben bar bya ba'i dus so//'dini rgyal rigs kyi 'khord kyi nang du 'gro ba'i dus so zhes bya ba nas/'di ni bram ze
73b8(1): dang/khyim bdag gi bar gyi 'khord kyi nang du 'gro pa'i dus so//'di lha dang/klu dang/gnod sbyin dang/dri za dang/lha ma yin dang/nam mkha' lding dang/myi 'am ci dang/lto 'phye chen po dang/
74a1(1): //brgya byin dang/tshangs pa dang/'jig rten skyong ba dang/dge slong dang/dge slong ma dang/dge bsnyen dang/dge bsnyen ma dang/dge bsnyen ma'i 'khor gyi nang du 'gro ba'i dus so/'di ni

74b1(1): khro bas gnond cing tha ba'i dri mnga' ba //de dag bde gshegs khyod kyi byams bas khyab//dge ba'i yon tan bral la log nges pa//myi mkhas rnam mang dag la khyod snying rje//bsod nams ye shes dge bas

75a10(1): dagbyung ngo//bud myed gang dag rab tu yid dga' sems dpa' bas/sil snyan yid du 'ong zhing snyan pa'i sgra byin tshe//phyogs bcu'i rgyal ba dam pa dag gi mthu' yis na//tshigs bcad rnam pa
75b1(1): sna tshogs rnam mang 'di dag byung//rtag tu mgon myed gyur pa'i 'gro pha 'di thos nas//gong phang mya ngan myed mchog rdul myed sangs rgyas te/skye dang rga dang sdug bsngal dag las grol lo zhes//dpa' bo

75b6(1): myi g.yo g.yo' ba myed//bde bar gshegs pa sngon mthar rgya chen gyurd//g.yo can bdud ni sde dang bcas btul te/ngan song gsum po tha dag bskams par gyis//gang phyir khyod
