75b9(1): pa mkhas/khyod ni ye shes mtha' yas dri myed che//rmongs pa yid nyis lam la gnas rnams la/shes rab 'od dge dam pa mdzes par gyis/khyod gyi srid pa brgyad ni byams pa spyod/snying rje
75b10(1): dang ni dga' mchog btang snyoms dang/khyod ni spyod pa dam gang spyad pa//spyod pa de nyid 'gro la rnam par phye/phyogs bcu rgyal ba dag gi gzi brjid kyis/yon tan me tog sna tshogs tshigs bcad
76a1(1): //'di/sil snyan dag las rnam mar yid 'ong 'grol//lam la 'dug pa'i gzhonu de la skul/gang gi tshe na dga' byed bud myed rab cu dga' pa rnams/shin tu mdzes shing rab tu snyoms pa'i sil
76a2(1): snyan sgrogs pa'i tshe/phyogs bcu dag gi rgyal ba lha myi 'dul ba mdzad rnams kyang/sil snyan dag las gsung mchog ha cang myi che rjes su sgrogs//'gro la phan phyir yon tan mang po ldan//khyod gang rnams su rnam
76a3(1): par rgyu ba'i tshe na/rgyal ba'i yon tan gnyug mar gnas//sngon gyi rtul zhugs dka' thub spyad pa dran bgyis te//shing mchog drung tu myur song 'chi myed go phang thob par gyis//lha myi dag gi shin tu sgom zhing rgyal
76a4(1): pa'i yon tan bral/khyod ni shin tu stobs ldan bdud rtsi'i ro bro'i rtag tu sbyin//stobs bcu ro bro dam pa mkhas pa rnams kyis mchod byas pa//bdud rtsi'i de ni myi bdag khyod kyis myur tu rnam par phye//rgyal
76a5(1): pa'i yon tan 'gro la phan par phyed la dga' ba po//khyod kyis sngon gyi srid par nor bu gser rnams btang//chung ma pu sdug dag dang grong khyer grong rdal bcas pa dang//rang gi mgo dang
76a6(1): dang myig dang lag pa dag dang rkang 'ang btang//myi mchog khyod ni gna' sngon rgyal po dge par gyurd pa'i tshe//myi zhig khyod la mngon tu phyogs nas 'di skad tshig smras te//grong khyer grong rdal
76a7(1): bcas pa'i sa 'di kho bos byin zhes byas//de'i tshe na rab tu dga' zhing 'khrug par gyur pa myed//gna' sngon khyod ni rgyal po bram ze dag par gyur pa'i tshe//skye bo bla ma bsnyen bkur byed cing gzhan la khu myi byed
76a8(1): bram ze dam pa dag dang skye bo mang po dge la bkod//de nas shi phos srid pa'i 'gro ba'i lha'i gnas su skyes//sngon khyod rgyal po'i bu las drang srong dam pa'i tshe//rgyal po ngan pas khros pas khyod kyi yan lag bcad pa na/khyod
76a9(1): ni yid 'khrugs gyur pa myed par 'chi ba'i dus byas te//de'i tshe na khyod kyi skal ga dag las 'o ma 'dzag//khyod sngon drang srong sngo bsangs zhes byar gang tshe gyurd pa na//ri mchog gnas na rtul zhugs dga' zhing bla ma la yang
76a10(1): dga'//myi'i bdag pos dug mnga' dag gis khyod la ma bsnun pa na/khyod ni rgyal po de la yid 'khrugs pa myed cing snying cher gyurd//khyod sngon ri dgas bdag po yon tan can du gyur pa'i tshe//ri bzar 'bab chu chen pos myi
76b1(1): khyer bar gyur pa na//phan dang ldan pa khyod kyis myi de phyung te btang la bzhag//khyod kyi sgra bo khrid de 'ongs pa na yang yid ma khrugs//myi mchog khyod sngon bram ze'i bu ni gyur pa na//khyod kyi nor bu rgya mtsho' chen po zab par bya bar rtsams
76b2(1): ba dang//stobs brtan khyu mchog khyod kyi nor bu de tshe phyir yang rnyed//skyes bu mchog khyod gna' sngon drang srong dam par gyur pa'i tshe//bram ze zhig gyis bdag gi skyabs 'gyur shog//ces khyod 'ga' 'ongs/bram ze dam pa
76b3(1): kho bo dgra las phyung zhes byas//khyod kyis rang lus btang zhing bram ze de ni ma btang ngo//gna' sngon shing gi gnas su drang srong sngo ba sangs drung 'ongs te//'dod pas smras pa/shing 'di lo ma du yod 'grangs shes byas shing
76b4(1): 'di lo ma ji snyed yod par rab grangs rab mkhas pa//khyod kyis de bzhin nor ba myed cing 'thun pa'i tshig tu brjod//khyod sngon ne tsor gyurd pa yon tan ldan gyur shing la gnas pa'i tshe//zad par gyurd kyang sngon gyi drin la bsams
76b5(1): te de ma spangs//lha'i dbang pos khyod kyi yon tan dran nas rab tu dga' snga ma kho bzhin du shing mchog de ni dpal dang ldan bar byas//khyod kyis brtul zhugs dka' thub spyad pa de dag mtshungs myed de//
76b6(1): yon tan ldan pa yon tan lam la spyad pas yon tan mar//khyod kyi dus la da bab grong khyer bcas pa'i sa yang mthong//grong pa dag ni rgyal ba'i yon tan spyod la myur tu khod//gang gi tshe na bud myed
76b7(1): bzang po'i gos lus brgyan te//sil snyan dam pa mchog rab tu yid du 'ong pa'i sgra ni rab tu sgrogs//de'i tshe phyogs bcu'i rgyal ba'i gzi' yis tshigs su bcad pa sna tshogs pa//sgra dbyangs snyand pa 'di dag
76b8(1): sil snyan dag sgra las rab tu byung//rga dang 'chi bas zind pa'i 'jig rten dag la bdag ni skyabs gyur ces//'jig rten sgron ma khyod kyis skal mang snga rol smon lam de skad btab//myi'i seng ge khyod kyis sngon gyi
76b9(1): smon lam dag ni dran gyis shig//rkang gnyis dbang po khyod da khyim nas byung ba'i dus ni 'di yin no//khyod 'dir srid pa khrag khrig dag tu sbyin pa mang po du ma byin//nod dang rin po che dang gser dang gos bzang rin cen
76b10(1): spras pa dang/rkang lag dag dang myig dang 'phangs pa'i bu dang rgyal srid 'byor pa dag//khyod kyis btang yang khyod ni slong ba rnams la tha ba zhe sdar myed//khyod 'dir zla ba'i tog dang mche ba bzang ba dang rgyal po zhi bar gyurd//
77a1(1): //snying rtse snying rje'i yid dang zla ba'i sgron dang gtsug na nor bur gyurd//rgyal po dbang po brtan dang myig bzangs dang ni de la stsogs pa byas/rgyal po khrag khrig mang po sbyin dga' rnam par 'phrul pa de nyid khyod
77a2(1): bde par shegs pa khyod kyis bskal pa mang po tshul khrims spyad pa spyod//nor bu rin cen dri ma myed pa lta bur tshul khrims rnam dag gyurd//khyod kyis 'bri'i rnga ma ci 'dra de 'drar tshul khrims srung shing spyad//tshul khrims dga' 
77a3(1): bas khyod kyis 'dir ni 'gro la rgya chen don yang byas//'dir khyod glang po mchog tu gyurd te sngon pa sgra yis mda' pa bsdud pa'i tshe/brtum po dgra la snying brtse snying rje bskyed nas de ni khyod kyis skyabs//bzang zhing mdzes pa'i che 
77a4(1): ba yongs su btang ste tshul khrims yong ma btang//khyod kyis de las stsogs pa'i tshul khrims rnam par 'phrul pa mang po byas//bzod la dga' bas khyod kyis 'gro ba'i myi phan sdug bsngal stong mang dang//tshig dag dang bsad
77a5(1): pa dag ni bcing ba mang po bzod par byas//khyod ni gna' sngon myi gang dag la bde ba kun kyi rim gror byas//de dag phyi nas khyod kyi shed mar gyurd kyang khyod kyis de dag bzod//'gon po khyod ni ri

81b3(1): btang ngo //dge slong dag de ltar byang chub sems dpa' btsun mo'i 'khor kyi nang na 'dug pa'i tshe yang //chos nyan pa dang bral bar ma gyur to//chos ma yin pa byed pa dang bral bar ma gyur te//de ci'i phyir zhe na//dge slong

81b5(1): dga' zhing mos par gyurd pa//chos tshol bas ngos sa myi shes pa//ji ltar thos pa'i chos yang dag par stond pa/bla na myed pa'i chen po'i sbyin bdag tu gyurd pa/zang zing myed par chos stond pa//

81b7(1): pa//chos la daphung gnyen du 'dzind pa/chos la brton pa/chos la nges par sems pa'i bzod pas nges par byung ba/shes rab kyi pha rold tu phyind pa la spyad pa ste/thabs la mkhas pa rtogs pa khong du chud pa yin no//dge 
81b8(1): slong dag de la byang chub sems dpa' thabs la mkhas pa chen pos rnam par brtse zhing /btsun mo'i 'khor thams cad kyi mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du spyod lam ni bstand/sngon gyi byang chub sems dpa'i 'jig rten gyi yul thams cad
81b9(1): las yang dag par 'das pa rnams kyi 'jig rten dang 'thun par byas pa'i chos nyid dang /mthun par ni byas//yun ring po nas 'dod pa'i skyon shin tu rtogs pa yin yang sems can yongs su smyind par bya ba'i dbang gis//'dod pa'i long 
81b10(1): spyod ni kun tu bstan pa dge ba'i rtsa ba dpag tu myed pa brtsags pa'i bsod nams kyi stobs kyi tshogs kyi khyad par gyis/'jig rten gyi dbang du bya ba mtshungs pa myed pa'i kun tu bstan//lha dang myi'i las shin tu 'das pa'i snying po dang 
82a1(1): @#//ldan zhing rgya che la rnam par tha dad pa dang/rnam pa sna tshogs kyi gzugs dang/sgra dang/dri dang/ro dang/reg mchog tu dga' ba nyams dga' ba'i 'dod pa'i dga' ba'i bde ba ni stand//'dod pa'i rga ba'i yul thaMs

82a3(1): 'jig rten gyi kun nas nyon mongs pa'i dri ma thams cad kyis ni sems la kun nas nyon mongs pa myed par btsun mo'i 'khor gyi nang na gnas te/ji ltar 'gron tu gnyer ba'i sems can gyi khams yongs su smyin par bya ba'i dus
82a4(1): la lta zhing/de'i tshe byang chub sems dpa' shin tu rgya cher sngon gyi dam bcas pa rjes su dran no//sangs rgyas dang chos kyang mngon tu byed do//smon lam gyi stobs kyang mngon par bsgrub po//sems can rnams la yang
82a5(1): snying rje chen po gtong ngo//sems can rab tu thar par yang sems so//'byor pa thams cad kyi mtha' ni rgud pa yin par yang so sor rtog go//'khor ba la gnod pa dang 'jigs pa mang po du ma yod

82a9(1): spyod yul dang ma 'dres pa yin/srid pa 'bar bar skyon du mthong pa yin/khams gsum pa las 'byung ba'i bsam pa can yin/'khor pa'i skyon dang nyes dmyigs dang/'byung pa la mkhas pa yin/rab tu 'byung par 'dod pa
82a10(1): yin/mngon par byung ba'i bsam pa can yin /dben pa la gzhol ba yin/dben pa la 'bab pa yin/mgon pa dang pas mthar mngon par dga' ba yin//rab tu dben pas rab tu zhi bar 'dod pa yin/bdag dang gzhan la phan
82b1(1): par zhugs par yin/bla na myed pa'i nan tan la dpa' ba yin/'jig rten gyi don du 'dod pa yin/phan par 'dod pa yin pa de bar 'dod pa yin/grub pa dang bde bar 'dod pa yin/'jig rten la snying brtse ba can yin//
82b2(1): phan bar 'dod pa can yin/byams pa la gnas pa can yin/snying rje chen po dang ldan pa yin/bsdu ba'i dngos po la mkhas pa yin/rtag tu yid yongs su myi skyo ba yin/sems can yongs su smyin par bya ba dang/'dul
82b3(1): ba la mkhas pa yin/sems can thams cad la bu cig dang 'dra bar sdug par sems pa yid la byed pa dang ldan pa yin/dngos po thams cad la myi lta bar yongs su gtong pa yin/sbyin ba 'gyed pa la dga' ba yin/lhug
82b4(1): par gtong ba yin/lag brkyang ba yin/rtog pa la dpa' pa yin/mchod sbyin byas pa yin/bsod nams shin tu phun gsum tshogs pa yin/bsod nams kyi yo byad shin tu bzung pa yin/dri ma dang ser sna dang
82b5(1): bral ba yin/sems can kun tu ma bzung pa yin/bla na myed pa'i sbyin bdag chen po yin/byin nas kyang rnam par smyin pa la myi re ba yin/sbyin pa la dpa' ba yin/'dod pa dang/'dod pa che ba dang/
82b6(1): chags pa dang/zhe sdang dang/rgyags dang/nga rgyal dang/gti mug dang/ser sna las lastsogs pa'i nyon mongs pa'i dgra'i tshogs phyir rgol pa thams cad tshar bcad pa'i phyir mngon bar byung ba yin/
82b7(1): thaMs cad mkhyen pa'i sems skyed pa'i rgyun las myi g.yo' ba yin ba gtor ba che ba'i sems kyi go cha shin tu bgos pa yin/'jig rten la snying che pa can yin/phan par 'dod pa can yin/ya lad bgos
82b8(1): pa yin/go cha bgos pa yin/sems can rab tu thar par bya ba la dmyigs pa yin/snying rje chen po'i stobs dang/rnam par gnon pa dang/pha rol la gnon pa dang ldan ba yin/phyir myi ldog pa yin/sems can
82b9(1): thams cad la sems mnyam ba yin/gtog pa'i lag cha can yin/sems can gyi bsam pa dang re ba ci lta ba bzhin du tshim par byed pa yin/byang chub kyi snod du gyur pa yin/dus dang phrad par chos rtogs pa yin/
82b10(1): smon lam byang chub tu yongs su bsngos pa yin/rgyal mtshan ma snyal pa yin/khor gsum yongs su dag pa'i sbyin pa yongs su gtong ba dang ldan ba yin/ye shes kyi mchog gi rdo rje'i mtshon cha sra ba dang ldan ba yin

84a1(1): //yongasu smyin bya'i sgra yang byung//dran pa nye par gzhag pa rab dbye sgra//yang dag spong dang de bzhin rdzu 'phrul sgra//dpang po lnga dang stobs lnga rab dbye dang/byang chub yan lag sgra ni sil snyan byung//yan lag

85b1(1): de nas rgyal bo gra shis kyi sgo'i sgo phar ched po byed du bcug go//sgo phar re re yang myi lnga brgya lnga brgyas 'byed cing bcad do//de dag gi sgra yang dpag tshad phyed du grag go/de la 'dod pa'i yon tan mtshungs pa

85b10(1): tog bsil ma ni mtshal bar bkram/pog phor nas ni gdugs bum gsang bas ni mdzes par byas/chu shing ljon pa ni bsgreng /dar tha dad pa sna tshogs kyi bla res ni bres/rin po che'i dril bu yer ka'i 
86a1(1): //'i dra ba dang/chun dkor dang chun pa ni spyangs/dpung bzhi'i bkod pa ni byas so/g.yog 'khor dang/gzho nu btsun mo'i 'khord brgyan pa'i phyir brtson bar gyurd to//de nas byang chub sems
86a2(1): ka dpa' bkod pa chen pos/grong khyer gyi shar phyogs kyi sgo nas/skyed mos tshal gyi sar byung ngo/de nas byang chub sems dpa' nyid kyi mthus gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu rnams kyis/myi rgad po gdug pa khogs
86a3(1): pa/lus las rtsa rgyus thond pa/so rgald pa/lus gnyer mas gang ba/skra byi ba/sgur ba/'dug rten ltar dgu ba/zhom ba/khar pa brten pa/mthu' zad pa/dar yal ba/dmyid pa nas dgra ngar ngar por 'byung
86a4(1): ba/lus bsgur nas 'khar pa la brten nas/yan lag dang nyid lag thams cad 'dar zhing lam gar bstan par gyurd to//de nas pyang chub sems dpas shes bzhin du/kha lo bsgyur pa la 'di skad ces smras

180b7(1): dang yid kyi las byams pa dang ldan pa'i phyir phyag ring ba zhes bya'o yun ring po nas zas kyi chod shes pa dang nyung du za ba dang lto sdom pa dang nad pa la sman sbyin pa dang skye bo dman pa la myi rnyas pa dang /dgon 
180b8(1): myed pa byang du myi gsod pa dang /de bzhin gshes pa'i mchod rten zhig pa chos pa dang /mchod rten rtsig pa dang /'jigs pas gnyen pa'i sems can rnaMs la myi 'jigs pa sbyin pa'i phyir shing n+ya ta ltar chu zheng gang 
180b9(1): pa zhes bya'o/yun ring por nas pha ma dang bram ze dang dge sbyong dang bla ma dang sbyin gnas rnams la bkru ba dang sku ba dang /mar dang 'bru mar gis skud pa dang grad pa'i dus na ni chu dron mo dang tsha ba'i dus na ni grang ba dang bsil 
180b10(1): grib dang dus kyi bde ba'i long spyod sbyin pa dang /shing bam 'jam po ltar reg na bde zhing 'dam ba'i ras legs par gting pa'i mal ma sbyin pa dang /de bzhin shegs pa'i mchod rten la spos kyi 'bru mar thor ba dang dar phra mo'i rgyal mtshan dang ba dan dang 
180b11(1): srad bu 'phul ba'i phyir sku'i khags pa 'jam zhing nyen la srab pa zhes bya'o/yum ring po nas sems can la khong khro ba mying byis pa bsgom pa la/sbyor ba dang bzod pa dang bde ba la sems can gzhan dag 'dzud pa dang /khon du 'dzind pa byed cing gnod sems myed pa'i 
180b12(1): yon tan gyi sngags pa brjod pa dang 
181a1(1): //de bzhin gshegs pa'i mchod rten dang/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku gzugs rnams gser gyi rgyand dang/gser gyi me tog dang/gser phye dor ba dang/gser gyi kha dog lta bu'i dar gyi ba dan dang/rgyal mtshan
181a2(1): dang rgyan dang/gser gi snod dang/gser gyi kha dog ltar bu'i gos dbul ba'i phyir pags pa'i mdog gser gyi kha dog lta bu zhes bya'o//yul rin po nas mkhas pa'i gan du 'gro zhing dge ba dang myi dge' ba gang yin zhes dri ba dang/kha na
181a3(1): mtho ba dang bcas ces pa dang/kha na mtho ba myed pa dang/bstand bar bya ba dang/bstend bar bya ba ma yin ba dang/ngan pa dang bring dang/gya nom ba'i chos yongasu 'dri ba dang/de'i don spyad pa zhes bya'o/dran ba dang blo gros dang
181a4(1): rtogs ba dang/blo phun gsum tshogs pa zhes bya'o/dran ba nye bar bzhag ba dang yang dag bar song dang/rdzu 'phrul gi rkang pa dang/dbang ba dang stobs dang byang chub kyi yan lag dang lam dang zhi gnas dang/lhag mthong gyis snang
181a5(1): ba bsnyes pa zhes bya'o/'khor ba'i rgya mtsho' las rgal ba zhes bya'o/pha rold tu byond pa zhes bya'o/thang la byond pa zhes bya'o/bde bar brnyes pa zhes bya'o/myi brnyes pa brnyes pa zhes bya'o/nyon mongs
181a6(1): pa'i tsher ma dkrugs pa zhes bya'o/skyes bu zhe bya'o/skyes bu chen po zhes bya'o/skyes bu seng ge zhes bya'o/'jigs pa dang su zin zhes byed pa myi mda' ba zhes bya'o/glang bo zhes bya'o/dri ma
181a7(1): myed pa zhes bya'o/dri ma sum spangs pa zhes bya'o/rig pa po zhes bya'o/rig pa gsum brnyes pa zhes bya'o/chu po bzhi las rgal ba zhes bya'o/rin po che 'i gdugs 'dzind pa cig bur gyurd pa'i phyir rgyal rigs
181a8(1): shes bya'o/sdig pa'i chos spangs pa'i phyir bram ze zhes bya'o/ma rig pa'i sgo nga'i sbu bas bcom pa'i phyir dge slong zhes bya'o/chags pa thams cad las yang dag bar 'das pa'i phyir dge sbyong zhes bya'o/nyon mongs
181a9(1): ba gzigs pa'i phyi gtsang sbra can zhes bya'o/stobs dang ldan ba zhes bya'o/stobs bcu mnga' ba zhes bya'o/bcom ldan 'das zhes bya'o/sku bsgoms pa zhes bya'o/rgyal po'i yang rgyal po zhes bya'o/chos kyi rgyal po
181a10(1): zhes bya'o/chos kyi 'khor lo mchog dam pa bskor zhing rjes su stond pa zhes bya'o/myi khrugs pa'i chos stond pa zhes bya'o/thams cad khyend pa'i ye shes kyi dbang skud pa zhes bya'o/chags pa myed pa'i ye shes chen po dri ma myed
181b1(1): cing rnam par grol ba'i go phang gi thabs brnyes pa zhes bya'o/byang chub kyi yan lag bdun gyi rin po che dang ldan pa zhes bya'o/chos rin po che thams cad kyi khyad par brnyes pa zhes bya'o/blon po 'phags pa'i nyan thos thams cad kyis zhal gyi
181b2(1): dkyil 'khor du bltas pa zhes bya'o/sras byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i 'khor can zhes bya'o/'dul ba shind tu btul ba zhes bya'o/byang chub sems dpa' legs par lung bstan pa zhes bya'o/nam thos kyi bu dang 'dra ba
181b3(1): zhes bya'o/'phags pa'i nor bdun gi mdzod byind pa zhes bya'o/gtong ba btang ba zhes bya'o/bde ba phun sum tshogs pa thams cad dang ldan ba zhes bya'o/re ba thams cad sbyin ba zhes bya'o/'jig rten thams cad la phan ba dang bde bas rjes
181b4(1): su skyong ba zhes bya'o/dbang bo dang tshungs pa zhes bya'o/ye shes mchog gi rdo rje 'chang ba zhes bya'o/kun nas sbyin zhes bya'o/chos thams cad la sgrib pa myi mnga' bar gzigs pa zhes bya'o/ye shes kun tu rnam par 'phrul ba
181b5(1): zhes bya'o/chos kyi gang gi rgyud rgya chen po stond pa la bzhugs pa zhes bya'o/zla ba dang chungs pa zhes bya'o/'gro ba thams cad kyis blta bas myi ngoms pa zhes bya'o/'od rnam par dag cing kun tu yangs pa zhes bya'o/
181b6(1): dga' ba dang mchog tu dga' bar mdzad pa'i 'od ces bya'o/sems can thams cad la mngon tu phyogs shing gzigs par snang ba zhes bya'o/'gro ba thams chad kyi sems dang bsam pa'i snod ci lta ba bzhin du snang
181b7(1): ba brnyes pa zhes bya'o/bkod pa chen po zhes bya'o/slob pa dang myi slob pa'i mgo rgyand sbyin pa'i phyir dbu' gtsug tor dang ldan zhing /spyi gtsug ltar myi thong ba zhes bya'o/yun ring po nas gtan pa myed pa'i mchod sbyin thams cad
181b8(1): byed pa dang/sems can thams cad dge ba yang dag par lend tu 'dzud pa dang/dge ba'i bshes gnyen gis rjes su bstand pa dang/'gal bar myi byed pa dang/chos smra ba rnaMs kyis mngags te phyogs rnaMs su 'gro ba dang/'ongs pas yongasu
181b9(1): myi skyo ba dang/sangs rgyas dang/byang chub sems dpa'/rang sangs rgyas dang/'phags pa nyan thos dang/pha ma dang/bla ma dang/sbyin nas thaMs cad la rim gro bya bar sbros sna tshogs kyi 'bru mar dang/mar dang sgron ma dang/mar me rnams
181b10(1): kyis/sun pa mun nag gsel pa dang/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku gzugs rnam pa'i tshig thaMs cad dang ldan ba mdzes pa byed du 'jug pa dang/'o ma lta bu'i mdzod spus brgyand pa byed pa dang/sems can gzhan byang chub kyi seMs mngon
182a1(1): //tu byed du 'jug pas/dge ba'i tshogs khyad bar du gyurd pa'i phyir skyid tshams kyi mdzod su mdzes par skyes shing g.yas su khril la 'bar zhi kha dog rnam par dag par snang ba dang /ldan ba zhes bya'o/sred myed kyi bu chen
182a2(1): bo'i stobs dang/ldan pa'i phyir mthu chen po brnyes pa zhes bya'o/bdud bye ba phrag rgya thuld pa'i stobs dang ldan pa'i phyir sred myed kyi bu chen po'i stobs dang ldan ba zhes bya'o de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu dang ldan pa'i phyir/phas
182a3(1): kyi rtsol pa thams cad 'dul ba zhes bya'o/gnas dang gnas ma yin ba khyind pa la khas pa dang /dman ba nyid tse ba'i theg pa rnam par sbod ba dang/theg pa chen po'i yon tan yang dag par bsgrub pa'i stobs dang ldan bas stobs la sbyor
182a4(1): bas/mchog myi shes pa'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa'i stoms bcu dang ldan ba zhes bya'o/'das pa dang ma 'ongs pa dang/da ltar byung ba'i las yang dag par blangs pa'i rgyud dang rnam par smyind par khyind pa'i stobs dang/ldan ba'i
182a5(1): phyir gnas dang gnas ma yin pa khyind pa la khas ma dang/dman ba nyi tse pa'i theg pa rnam par sbong ba dang/theg pa chen po'i yin tan yang dag par sgrub pa'i stobs dang ldan pas stobs la sbyor bas mchog myi
182a6(1): shes pa zhes bya'o/sems can thams cad kyi dbang po dang/brtson 'grus kyi rim pa khyind pa'i stobs dang ldan pa'i phyir 'das pa dang ma 'ongs ba dang/da ltar byung ba'i lam yang dag par blangs pa'i rgyad dang rnam par smind
182a7(1): pa khyind pa'i stobs dang ltan ba zhes bye'o//bsaM du ma dang khams sna tshogs pa'i 'jig rten la 'jug ba khyend pa'i stobs dang ldan ba'i phyir sems can thaMs cad kyi dbang po dang /brtson 'grus kyi rim ba khyind pa'i stobs
182a8(1): dang ldan ba zhes bya'o/mos pa du ma dang/mos pa sna tshogs dang/mos pa ma lus pa dang/rnam par grol ba khyind pa'i stobs dang lhan pa'i phyir khams du ma dang khams sna tshogasapa'i 'jig rten la 'jug pa'i stobs dang
182a9(1): ldan ba zhes bya'o/thams cad du 'gro pa'i lam khyind pa'i stobs dang lan ba'i phyir mos pa du ma dang/mos par sna tshogs dang/mos pa lus pa thams cad dang/rnam par grol ba mkhyend pa'i stobs dang ldan
182a10(1): bya'o//bsam gtan dang/rnam par thard pa dang/ting nge 'dzind dang/snyoms bar 'jug pa'i kun nas nyon mongs pa dang/rnam bar byang ba dang rnam bar bzhag pa dang thams cad mkhyend pa'i stobs dang dang ldan pa'i phyir
182b1(1): thams cad du 'gro ba'i lam khyedad pa'i stobs dang ldan ba zhes bya'o/sngon gyi gnas rnam pa du ma dran ba khyend pa chags pa myi mnga' pa'i stobs dang ldan pa'i phyir/bsam gtan dang/rnam par thard pa dang/ting nge 'dzind
182b2(1): dang/snyoms bar 'jug pa'i kun nas nyon mongs pa dang/rnam par byang ba dang/rnam bar bzhag pa thams cad khyend pa'i stobs dang ldan bzhes bya'o/gzugs ma lus ba thams cad sgrib pa myed par gzigs pa'i
182b3(1): lha'i spyan gyis mkhyend pa'i stobs dang ldanapa'i phyir/sngon gyi gnas rnam pa ru ma dran ba mkhyend pa'i chags pa myi mnga' pa'i stongs dang ldan bzhes bya'o/bag chags kyi tshams sbyor bar 'gyur ba thams cad dang/zag pa
182b4(1): thams cad ma lus bar zad pa mkhyend pa'i stobs dang lan ba'i phyir/gzugs ma lus bar thams cad sgrib ba myed bar gzigs pa'i lha'i spyan gyi mkhyend pa'i stobs dang lngan bzhes bya'o/chos thams cad ma lus bar
182b5(1): mngon bar rdzogs bar sangs rgyas so zhes dam bcas pa la lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten gyis brild kyis myi non pa'i dam bcas pa'i myi 'jigs pa rnyes ba'i phyir/bag chags kyi mtsham ba sbyor ba 'gyur
182b6(1): ba thams cad dang/zag thams cad ma lus bar zadapa ma khyend pa'i stobs dang ldan ba zhes bya'o//kun nas nyon mongs pa/thams cad mya ngan 'das pa'i bar du gcod ba'i chos su bstand te/der
182b7(1): dam bcas pa la lha dang bachas pa'i 'jig rten gyis/myi shigs pa'i dam bcas pa'i spyi 'jigs brnyes pa'i phyir chos thams cad ma lus par mngon bar rdzogs bar sangasargyas so zhes dam bcas pal/lha dang bcas
182b8(1): ba'i 'jig rten gyi zild kyis myi non pa'i dam pa bcas ba'i spyi gzigs pa brnyes pa zhes bya'o/nges bar 'byung pa'i lam bsgrubs na/mya ngan las 'das pa brnyed bar 'gyur ro zhes dam bcas pa la lha bar bcas
182b9(1): pa'i 'jig rten gyis brgal du myed pa'i dam bcas pa'i mi 'jigs pa brnyes pa'i phyir kun nas nyon mongs pa thams cad mya ngan las 'das pa'i bar du gcodapa'i chos su bstand te/de dam
183a1(1): //pa mkhyend par dam bcas par la lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rtend gis myi zlog pa'i dam bcas pa'i myi 'jigs pa brnyes pa'i phyir/nges par 'byung ba'i lam bsgrubs na mya las 'das pa brnyed par 'gyur ro zhes
183a2(1): dam bcas pa la lha dang bcas pa'i 'ji rten gis brgal du myed pa dam bcas pa'i myi 'jig pa brnyes pa zhes bya'o/tshig ma 'khrul par chos bstond pa'i phyir zag pa thams cad spangs pa mkhyend par dam bcas la la lha dang bcas pa
183a3(1): 'i 'jig rten gis myi zlos pa'i dam bcas pa'i mi 'jigs pa brnyes pa zhes bya'o//bskar ma'i tshogs kyi 'khor can zhes bya'o/nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dang tshungs pa zhes bya'o/mun pa mun nag rtsald pa zhes bya'o/tog chen po dang dang
183a4(1): ldan pa'i rgyal po zhes bya'o/'od zer tshad myed cing mtha' yas pa zhes bya'o/snang ba chen po kun tu stond pa zhes bya'o/dris pa thams cad lung bstand pa bston pa la rmongs pa myi mnga' pa zhes bya'o/ma rig pa'i mun pa chen po 'jom
183a5(1): par 'dzad pa zhes bya'o/ye shes kyi snang ba chen pos rnam par gzigs pa'i blo dang ldan pa zhes bya'o/rnam par myi rtog pa zhes bya'o/byams pa dang snying rtse pa dang snying rje chen pos dgro pa thams cad la 'od zer ma
183a6(1): nyam par gtong zhing yul tshad myed pa zhes bya'o//shes rab kyi pha rold tu phyind pa zab cing brnyed par dka' pa'i dkyil khor can zhes bya'o/tshangs pa dang tshungs pa zhes bya'o//rab tu zhi ba'i spyod lam
183a7(1): dang ldan pa zhes bya'o//spyod lam dang spyod pa'i khyad par thams cad dang ldan pa zhes bya'o//gzugs pa mnga' ba zhes bya'o//lta bas myi ngoms pa zhes bya'o/mang po zhi ba zhes bya'o/yid zhi ba zhes bya'o/zhi gnas kyi tshogs
183a8(1): yongs su rdzogs pa zhes bya'o/zhi gnas dam pa snyes pa zhes bya'o/dul ba dang zhi gnas mchog brnyes pa zhes bya'o/zhi gnas rang lhag mthong gi tshogs yongs su rdzogs pa zhes pa'o/spos pa dang dbang po thuld pa dang/glang
183a9(1): po ltar shin tu dul ba dang 'tsho ltar brnyog pa myed cing dang ba zhes bya'o/nyon mongs pa'i bag chags kyi sgrib pa thams cad legs par spangs pa zhes bya'o/skyes bu chen po'i mtshan gsum bcu brtsa nyis dang ldan pa zhes bya'o/skyes
183a10(1): bu chen po'i ces bya'o/dpe' bya bzang po brgyad bcu'i 'khor sna tshogs kyis sku sprul pa zhes bya'o/skyes bu chenapo'i khyu mchog ces bya'o/bstobs bcu dang ldan pa zhes bya'o/myi 'jigs pa bzhi rnyes pa zhes bya'o/skyes bu 'dul
183b1(1): kha lo sgyurd pa bla na myed pa zhes bya'o /ston pa zhes bya'o /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bcob brgyad yongs su rdzogs pa zhes bya'o /sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi 'phrin las kyi tha ma sman pa zhes bya'o /rnam pa'i mchog thaMs 
183b2(1): cad dang ldan pa'i phyir shind tu yongs su sbyangs pa'i ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor mnga' ba zhes bya'o /rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba mnyam pa nyid du mngon par thugs su chud pa 'i phyird stong pa nyid la gnas pa zhes bya'o /don dam pa'i
183b3(1): bdan pa tshul rab tu rtogs pa'i phyir mtshan ma myed pa la gnas pa zhes bya'o /'jug pa thams cad kyis myi 'gos pa'i phyir smon pa myed pa la gnas pa zhes ba'o /'du byed thams cad kyi rgyu gcad phyir mngon par 
183b4(1): 'du mdzad pa myi mnga' ba zhes bya'o /yang dag pa'i mtha'i ye shes kyi yul rnam par myi 'khrugs pa'i phyir yang dag pa gsung ba zhes bya'o /de bzhin nyid dang chos kyi dbyings dang nam mkha'i mtshan nyid rnam par shes pa'i 
183b5(1): yul yin pa'i phyir ma nor ba'i de bzhin nyid dang /gzhan ma yin pa gsung ba zhes bya'o /chos thaMs can sgyu ma dang smyig rgyu dang rmi lam dang chu lha dang brag cha dang myig g.yor dang tshungs par gnas 
183b6(1): pa'i phyir nyon mongs pa myed pa'i chos shin tu rtogs pa zhes bya'o /yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa'i rgyu skye pa'i phyir mthong ba don yod pa zhes bya'o /sems can 'dul bar btul ba'i rtsal dang ldan
183b7(1): pa'i gos ma dor ba don yod zhes bya'o /ma rig pa dang srid pa'i srid pa yang dag pa yang dag par bcad par 'i phyir 'dos pa las brgal ba zhes bya'o /nges par 'byung ba'i lam legs par bstan pa'i phyir zas ma btags pa zhes bya'o /
183b8(1): bdud kyi yul dang /spyod pa thams cad kyis myi gos pa'i phyir /bdud dang nyon mongs pa 'i phyir brgol ba las rgyal pa zhes bya'o /'dod pa'i khams las yang dag par 'das pa'i rgal pa zhes bya'o /gzugs kyi 
183b9(1): khams las yang dag par 'dus pa'i phyir nga rgyal gyi rgyal tshan bsnyal ba zhes bya'o /gzugs myed pa'i khams las yang dgapar 'das pa'i phyir shes rab kyi brgyal mtshan sgreng ba zhes bya'o /chos kyi sku dang ye shes kyi 
183b10(1): sku dang ldan pa'i phyir 'jig rtend gi yul thaMs cad las yang dag par 'das ba zhes bya'o /yon tan rin po cen 'i ye shes kyi me to mtha' yas pa shin tu rgyas shing rnaM par grol ba'i 'bras bu shin tu phun suM tshogs pa'i phyir sdong po chen po
184a1(1): //'byung ba dang thong ba de dka' ba'i phyir u dum 'bar ra'i me tog dang tshungs pa zhes bya 'o /tshul ci lta ba bzhin du nges par 'byung pa'i bsam pa shin tu yongasu rdzogs par mdzad pa'i phyir yid bzhin gi nor bu rin po che'i 
184a2(1): rgyal po dang tshungs pa zhes bya'o /yun ring po nas gtang pa dang tshul khrims dang dka' thub dang /rtul zhugs dang tshangs par spyod par brtan por yang dag par blangs pas myi g.yo' zhing myi bskyod pa'i phyir zhabs shin tu gnas pa zhes bya'o /
184a3(1): yun ring po nas pha ma dang dge sbyong dang bram ze dang bla ma dang /sbyin gnas rnams dang chos dang ldan pa yongs su skyong pa dang skyabs su lhags pa rnams yongasu myi gtong pa'i phyir zhabs kyi thil kra shis dang yung rung khyil pa dang 'khor lo brtsibs stong dang ldan pa 
184a4(1): sna tshogs kyi mtshan dang ldan pa zhes bya'o/yun ring po nas srog gcod pa sangs pa'i phyir zhabs gting pa yangs pa zhes bya'o /yun rin po nas sems can gzhan srog gcod pa spang pa yang dag par 'dzind tu 'jug pa'i phyir phyag gi sor mo 
184a5(1): ring pa zhes bya'o /yun ring pa nas srog gcod pa spong pa'i yon tan gi ba sngangs pa brjod pa 'dzad pa dang /skye bo mang po'i bskyabs mdzad pa'i phyird sku che zhing drang ba zhes bya'o //yun ring po nas pha ma dad dge sbyong dang bram
184a6(1): ze dang bla ma dang /sbyin nas la bsnyen bkur ba dang /yongasu spyod pa byed bkru ba dang byug pa dang mar dang 'bru mar gis skud cing lag dar te /lus la sbyar ba byed pas yongs su myi skyo ba'i phyir phyag dang zhabs 'jam zhing mnyen pa dang 
184a7(1): ldan zhes bya'o /bsdu ba'i dngos po bzhi' bo sbyin pa dang snyan par smra ba dang /don byed pa dang /don thun pa'i dra bas sems can bsdu' pha la mkhas pa legs bslabs pa'i phyir phyag dang zhabs dra bas 'brel pa zhes bya'o /yun rin po nas grong nas
184a8(1): grong du dge ba'i rtsa ba shin tu 'phags pa la lhag par dmigs pa'i phyir zhabs tho' ba zhes bya'o /yun ring po nas pha ma dang dge sbyong dang bram ze dang sbyin bdag dang de bzhin gshegs pa'i mchod rten la bskor ba byed pa dang /lus kyi las dang gus par 
184a9(1): chos nyan cing spu zin zhes byed pa dang /sems can gzhan yang dag par dga' bar byed pa dang /yongs su 'jal ba la rmongs pa myed pa dang ////de bzhin gshegs pa'i mchodarten la srin bu kyi ta dang /lu ta'i tshal dang /ba rgya dang 
184a10(1): me tog rnying pa dang /rtsa sna tshogs dang /phyag dar yod pa gsel par sbyord ba'i phyir spu re re skyes pa zhes bya'o /yun ring bo nas sems can /gtso bo dang /dam pa dang /mchod pa'i 'os dang /dge sbyang dang /bram ze dang /'dongs pa dang /slong pa la stsogs

204a6(1): phyin ci ma log pa nyid myed de/gsog kyang de nyid yin la/gsob kyang de nyid yin/snying po myed pa yang de nyid yin/brdzun ba yang de nyid yin/slu ba'i chos kyang de nyid yin
204a7(1): bdud kyang de nyid yin/bdud kyi yo byad kyang de nyid yin/ro yang de nyid yin/ro'i gzugs kyang de nyid yin pas/de la ni mngon bar dga' bar bya ba 'am/yongs su rdzogs par bya ba 'am/lhag par 
204a8(1): chags par bya ba 'am/lhag bar chags shing gnas par bya ba myi rigs kyi/bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa/'phags pa 'jig rten las 'das pa ni bden no zhes de ltar so sor rtog par byed do//des 
204a9(1): de ltar shes nas/yid kyi rnam par shes par bya ba 'das pa dang/ma 'ongs pa dang/da ltar byung pa'i chos rnams nad du yang dag par rjes su mthong zhing/'bras dang/zug rngu dang/sdig pa dang/
204a10(1): myi rtag pa dang/sdug bslang ba dang/stong pa dang/bdag myed par yang dag par rjes su ma thong/des yid kyi rnam par shes par ba ba 'das pa dang/ma 'odas pa dang/da ltar byung ba'i chos rnams nad du yang
204b1(1): dag par rjes su mthong zhing/'bras dang zug rngu dang/sdig pa dang/myi rtag pa dang/sdug bsngal pa dang/stong pa dang/bdag myed par yang dag par rjes su mthong ba na/de'i yid kyi rnam par shes par
204b2(1): bya ba 'das pa dang/ma 'ongs pa dang/da ltar byung pa'i chos dag log par mos pa 'am/'du shes phyin ci log gam/sems phyin ci log gam/lta ba phyin ci log tu spyad pa /'di lta ste/brnab
204b3(1): sems kyi tshogs kyi mdud pa dang/gnod sems kyi tshogs kyi mdud la dang/tshul khrims dang/brtul zhugs mchog tu 'dzind pa'i tshogs kyi mdud pa dang /'di bden no snyam du mngon par zhen cing
204b4(1): mchog tu 'dzin pa'i tshogs kyi mdud pa gang yin ba de dag spong bar byed do/de dag spangs pa'i phyir bdud mdongs pa dang/yi chad pa dang/phyogs myed par bya ste/gnas shing bdud sdig can gyis
204b5(1): myi mthong bar song nas/bdud dang bdud kyi 'khor bcom zhing g.yul tshon pho'i g.yul spyad do/g.yul las rnam par rgyal ba dang/g.yul ngo de nyid
204b6(1): mngon bar pham par byas shing gnas te//de ltar na 'di la 'phags pa nyan thos lhag pa'i shes rab la/brtson pa'i shes rab rjes su sbyor ba la
204b7(1): gnas pa ni/shes rab kyis tsho ba rnams kyi nang na bla na myed pas 'tsho ba yin no//bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' bstsald nas/dge slong dag yid rangs ste/bcom
204b8(1): ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa la mngon par bstod do//bar gyi sdom ni son pa dang ni sgyu ma dang/ra ro pa dang sgra brnyan dang/shed ma dang ni me long dang/zas sbyin dang ni smyig
204b9(1): rgyu dang/rkun po gtses pa grong gnas dang/rmi lam dga' spyod za ma tog//nor bu daphung rgyan rnams dag ni//sgyu ma 'dra ba'i kun bsdus pa'o//mdo' chen po sgyu ma dra ba zhes
